d(e);e.addEventListener('click',ev=>{if(ev.target===e)close()});e.querySelector('#v65Close').onclick=close;
 e.addEventListener('keydown',ev=>{if(ev.key==='Escape'){ev.preventDefault();close()}if(ev.key!=='Tab')return;const a=[...e.querySelectorAll('button:not([disabled]),input:not([disabled]),textarea:not([disabled]),select:not([disabled]),summary')].filter(n=>n.getClientRects().length&&!n.closest('[hidden]'));if(!a.length)return;let first=a[0],last=a[a.length-1];if(ev.shiftKey&&document.activeElement===first){ev.preventDefault();last.focus()}else if(!ev.shiftKey&&document.activeElement===last){ev.preventDefault();first.focus()}});
 return e;}
function open(){const e=makeShell();prevFocus=document.activeElement;e.classList.add('open');document.body.classList.add('v65-freeze');e.querySelector('.v65-shell').focus();}
function close(){const e=document.getElementById('v65Layer');if(!e?.classList.contains('open'))return;e.classList.remove('open');document.body.classList.remove('v65-freeze');cur=null;quiz=null;if(prevFocus?.isConnected&&prevFocus.getClientRects().length)prevFocus.focus()}
function show(html){let e=makeShell();e.querySelector('#v65Body').innerHTML=html;e.querySelector('.v65-shell').scrollTop=0;}
function choiceButtons(q,kind){const order=[...q.options.keys()];for(let j=order.length-1;j>0;j--){const r=Math.floor(Math.random()*(j+1));[order[j],order[r]]=[order[r],order[j]]}return `<div class="v65-options">${order.map((n,i)=>`<button type="button" data-v65-option="${n}" data-v65-kind="${kind}"><i>${String.fromCharCode(65+i)}</i><span>${esc(q.options[n])}</span></button>`).join('')}</div>`}
function intro(){open();mode='guide';const saved=read(K);const last=(saved.draft>0)?DISC.missions.find(m=>m.id===saved.current):null;
 const arranged=[...DISC.missions].sort((a,b)=>{const past=saved.finished||{};return (past[a.id]?.count||0)-(past[b.id]?.count||0)});const primary=[],extra=[];const seenSubjects=new Set();for(const item of arranged){if(!seenSubjects.has(item.subject)){seenSubjects.add(item.subject);primary.push(item)}else extra.push(item)}
 show(`<main class="v65-main"><span class="v65-kicker">A LITTLE CURIOSITY, A REAL RESULT</span><h1>One good learning session.</h1><p class="v65-lead">We'll start with an interesting idea, try it somewhere new, and explain it in your own words. No clocks, no streaks, no pressure to finish.</p>${last?`<button id="v65Resume" type="button" class="v65-resume">↗ Continue: <strong>${esc(last.title)}</strong></button>`:''}
 <div class="v65-pick-header"><h2>What sounds interesting?</h2><button id="v65Surprise" type="button" class="v65-link">Pick for me →</button></div><div class="v65-grid">${primary.map(m=>`<button type="button" data-v65-mission="${esc(m.id)}"><strong>${esc(m.subject)}</strong><span>${esc(m.title)}</span></button>`).join('')}</div><details class="v65-topic-more"><summary>See more starting ideas</summary><div class="v65-grid">${extra.map(m=>`<button type="button" data-v65-mission="${esc(m.id)}"><strong>${esc(m.subject)}</strong><span>${esc(m.title)}</span></button>`).join('')}</div></details>
 <div class="v65-sidebar"><div><b>Spanish is still confusing?</b><p>Get comfortable with present, completed past, and past habits. Type actual verbs instead of choosing an answer.</p></div><button id="v65ToVerbs" type="button" class="v65-secondary">Open the Practice Spanish verbs →</button></div></main>`);
 document.querySelectorAll('[data-v65-mission]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>begin(b.dataset.v65Mission));document.getElementById('v65Surprise').onclick=()=>begin(arranged[Math.floor(Math.random()*Math.min(arranged.length,8))].id);const r=document.getElementById('v65Resume');if(r)r.onclick=()=>begin(last.id,true);document.getElementById('v65ToVerbs').onclick=()=>verbIntro();}
function persistGuide(){if(!cur)return;const d=read(K);d.current=cur.id;d.draft=cur.step;d.draftNotes=cur.notes.slice(0,4000);d.updated=Date.now();write(K,d)}
function begin(id,resume=false){const m=DISC.missions.find(x=>x.id===id);if(!m)return intro();open();mode='guide';cur={id,m,step:resume?Math.min(3,Math.max(0,read(K).draft||0)):0,help:false,wrong:false,q1:false,q2:false,notes:resume?(read(K).draftNotes||''):'',criteria:[],review:false};if(cur.step===0)story();else if(cur.step===1)firstQuestion();else if(cur.step===2)freshQuestion();else reflection();}
function frame(kicker,heading,sub,inner){return `<main class="v65-main"><div class="v65-step"><button class="v65-link" id="v65Back" type="button">← Change subject</button><span>${esc(kicker)}</span></div><h1>${esc(heading)}</h1>${sub?`<p class="v65-lead">${esc(sub)}</p>`:''}${inner}</main>`}
function bindBack(){document.getElementById('v65Back').onclick=intro}
function story(){cur.step=0;persistGuide();const m=cur.m;show(frame('01 / NOTICE','An idea worth understanding',m.tag,`<section class="v65-story"><span>PICTURE THIS</span><p>${esc(m.hook)}</p></section><h2>${esc(m.title)}</h2><p class="v65-bigtext">${esc(m.idea)}</p><div class="v65-example"><span>SEE IT WORK</span><p>${esc(m.example)}</p></div><div class="v65-actions"><button class="v65-primary" id="v65First" type="button">Let me try →</button><small>You can always return to the explanation.</small></div>`));bindBack();document.getElementById('v65First').onclick=firstQuestion}
function question(kind){if(!cur)return;const m=cur.m;if(kind===1){cur.step=1;persistGuide();const q=m.questions[0];show(frame('02 / MAKE A CHOICE','What would you do?',m.title,`<div class="v65-prompt"><p>${esc(q.ask)}</p></div>${choiceButtons(q,'one')}<div class="v65-feedback" id="v65Feedback" role="status" hidden></div><div class="v65-help"><button id="v65Hint" class="v65-link" type="button">Break it down</button><button id="v65Reveal" class="v65-link" type="button">Show me how</button></div><div class="v65-actions" id="v65NextWrap" hidden><button id="v65Next" class="v65-primary" type="button">Try a different situation →</button></div>`));bindBack();bindChoice(q,()=>{cur.q1=true;const x=document.getElementById('v65NextWrap');if(x)x.hidden=false},'one');document.getElementById('v65Next').onclick=freshQuestion;}
 else {cur.step=2;persistGuide();const raw=TRANS.BANK[cur.id]?.[0];if(!raw)return reflection();const q={options:raw.opts,correct:raw.correct,ask:raw.ask,feedback:raw.why,hint:raw.hint};show(frame('03 / APPLY IT','Same idea. Different setting.',`If it works here too, the idea is becoming more useful.`, `<div class="v65-prompt"><p>${esc(q.ask)}</p></div>${choiceButtons(q,'two')}<div class="v65-feedback" id="v65Feedback" role="status" hidden></div><div class="v65-help"><button id="v65Hint" class="v65-link" type="button">Make this smaller</button><button id="v65Reveal" class="v65-link" type="button">Walk me through it</button></div><div class="v65-actions" id="v65NextWrap" hidden><button id="v65Next" class="v65-primary" type="button">Explain it in my own words →</button></div>`));bindBack();bindChoice(q,()=>{cur.q2=true;document.getElementById('v65NextWrap').hidden=false},'two');document.getElementById('v65Next').onclick=reflection;}}
function firstQuestion(){question(1)}function freshQuestion(){question(2)}
function bindChoice(q,complete,kind){let done=false,wrong=false,help=false;const feedback=document.getElementById('v65Feedback');const helpRow=document.querySelector('.v65-help');function post(head,detail,css){feedback.hidden=false;feedback.className='v65-feedback '+css;feedback.innerHTML=`<strong>${esc(head)}</strong><p>${esc(detail)}</p>`}
 function finish(assisted){if(done)return;done=true;cur.help=cur.help||assisted;cur.wrong=cur.wrong||wrong;document.querySelectorAll(`[data-v65-kind="${kind}"]`).forEach(b=>{b.disabled=true;b.classList.toggle('right',Number(b.dataset.v65Option)===q.correct)});helpRow.hidden=true;post('Here’s why it works',q.feedback[q.correct]+(assisted?' You can return to this later to see what sticks.':' You found this answer without help.'),'solved');complete()}
 document.querySelectorAll(`[data-v65-kind="${kind}"]`).forEach(btn=>btn.onclick=()=>{if(done)return;const n=Number(btn.dataset.v65Option);if(n===q.correct)finish(wrong||help);else{wrong=true;cur.wrong=true;btn.disabled=true;btn.classList.add('wrong');post('That answer is tempting, but…',q.feedback[n]+ ' Try another choice.', 'miss')}});
 document.getElementById('v65Hint').onclick=()=>{if(done)return;help=true;cur.help=true;post('One smaller step',q.hint,'hint')};document.getElementById('v65Reveal').onclick=()=>{help=true;cur.help=true;finish(true)};
}
function reflection(){cur.step=3;persistGuide();const b=EXPLAIN.BANK[cur.id];if(!b)return endGuide();show(frame('04 / EXPLAIN IT','Say it in your own words.',`You can write one or two sentences. The goal isn't a perfect essay; it's to find out what makes sense.`, `<section class="v65-prompt"><p>${esc(b.prompt)}</p></section><label class="v65-label" for="v65Text">YOUR EXPLANATION</label><textarea id="v65Text" rows="5" placeholder="Try a rough answer. You can edit it after seeing the explanation.">${esc(cur.notes)}</textarea><div class="v65-help"><button id="v65Starter" type="button" class="v65-link">Give me a sentence starter</button><button id="v65Small" type="button" class="v65-link">Ask something easier</button></div><p class="v65-feedback" id="v65WritingHint" hidden></p><div class="v65-actions"><button id="v65Compare" class="v65-primary" type="button">Compare with a worked explanation →</button><small>No automatic grade. Your notes stay on this device.</small></div>`));bindBack();const t=document.getElementById('v65Text');t.addEventListener('input',()=>{cur.notes=t.value.slice(0,4000);persistGuide()});document.getElementById('v65Starter').onclick=()=>{cur.help=true;const p=document.getElementById('v65WritingHint');p.hidden=false;p.textContent='Start here: '+b.starter};document.getElementById('v65Small').onclick=()=>{cur.help=true;const p=document.getElementById('v65WritingHint');p.hidden=false;p.textContent=b.small};document.getElementById('v65Compare').onclick=()=>{cur.review=true;compare()};}
function compare(){const b=EXPLAIN.BANK[cur.id];show(frame('05 / CHECK YOUR THINKING','What did you already understand?',`Read the example. Then check only the ideas your original explanation really covered.`, `<div class="v65-compare"><span>YOUR ATTEMPT</span><p>${esc(cur.notes.trim()||'[You chose to read the explanation first.]')}</p></div><div class="v65-model"><span>ONE CLEAR EXPLANATION</span><p>${esc(b.model)}</p></div><div class="v65-pitfall"><b>Common trap</b><p>${esc(b.pitfall)}</p></div><fieldset class="v65-checks"><legend>What was in your own explanation?</legend>${b.criteria.map((c,i)=>`<label><input type="checkbox" data-v65-check="${i}" ${cur.criteria.includes(i)?'checked':''}><span><b>${esc(c[0])}</b><small>${esc(c[1])}</small></span></label>`).join('')}</fieldset><div class="v65-actions"><button id="v65Finish" class="v65-primary" type="button">Finish this session →</button><button id="v65Edit" class="v65-secondary" type="button">Improve my explanation</button></div>`));bindBack();document.querySelectorAll('[data-v65-check]').forEach(i=>i.onchange=()=>{const n=+i.dataset.v65Check;cur.criteria=i.checked?[...new Set([...cur.criteria,n])]:cur.criteria.filter(x=>x!==n)});document.getElementById('v65Edit').onclick=reflection;document.getElementById('v65Finish').onclick=endGuide;}
function endGuide(){if(!cur)return;const d=read(K);d.current=cur.id;d.draft=0;d.draftNotes='';d.finished=d.finished||{};let r=d.finished[cur.id]||{count:0};r.count=(r.count||0)+1;r.last=Date.now();r.guided=Boolean(cur.help||cur.wrong||!cur.notes.trim());r.covered=cur.criteria.length;r.notes=cur.notes.slice(0,4000);d.finished[cur.id]=r;d.updated=Date.now();write(K,d);
 const finished=DISC.missions.filter(m=>(d.finished[m.id]?.count||0)>0).length;const next=DISC.missions.find(m=>m.subject===cur.m.subject&&m.id!==cur.id)||DISC.missions.find(m=>!d.finished[m.id]&&m.id!==cur.id);
 show(`<main class="v65-main v65-ending"><span class="v65-kicker">A GOOD PLACE TO STOP</span><h1>You did more than recognize an answer.</h1><p class="v65-lead">You encountered an idea, applied it in a different setting, and looked critically at an explanation. You're getting a feel for it. Come back later and see what you remember.</p><div class="v65-recap"><b>${esc(cur.m.title)}</b><span>${cur.notes.trim()?'Your own explanation is saved.':'You studied the worked explanation.'}</span><span>${cur.criteria.length} of 3 ideas self-checked · ${finished} of 16 topics explored in this room</span></div><div class="v65-actions">${next?`<button class="v65-primary" id="v65NextTopic" type="button">Another interesting idea →</button>`:''}<button class="v65-secondary" id="v65Leave" type="button">I'm done for now</button></div><p class="v65-smallprint">To test lasting recall, come back tomorrow and use “Test what stuck” from the original course.</p></main>`);
 const n=document.getElementById('v65NextTopic');if(n)n.onclick=()=>begin(next.id);document.getElementById('v65Leave').onclick=close;}
// Spanish conjugation lab: explicit regular tables plus vetted frequent irregular forms.
const subjects=[['yo','I'],['tú','you'],['ella','she'],['nosotros','we'],['ellos','they']];
const endings={present:{ar:['o','as','a','amos','an'],er:['o','es','e','emos','en'],ir:['o','es','e','imos','en']},preterite:{ar:['é','aste','ó','amos','aron'],er:['í','iste','ió','imos','ieron'],ir:['í','iste','ió','imos','ieron']},imperfect:{ar:['aba','abas','aba','ábamos','aban'],er:['ía','ías','ía','íamos','ían'],ir:['ía','ías','ía','íamos','ían']}};
const irregular={
 ser:{present:['soy','eres','es','somos','son'],preterite:['fui','fuiste','fue','fuimos','fueron'],imperfect:['era','eras','era','éramos','eran']},
 ir:{present:['voy','vas','va','vamos','van'],preterite:['fui','fuiste','fue','fuimos','fueron'],imperfect:['iba','ibas','iba','íbamos','iban']},
 estar:{present:['estoy','estás','está','estamos','están'],preterite:['estuve','estuviste','estuvo','estuvimos','estuvieron'],imperfect:['estaba','estabas','estaba','estábamos','estaban']},
 tener:{present:['tengo','tienes','tiene','tenemos','tienen'],preterite:['tuve','tuviste','tuvo','tuvimos','tuvieron'],imperfect:['tenía','tenías','tenía','teníamos','tenían']}
};
const verbs=[['hablar','speak','con amigos'],['estudiar','study','español'],['beber','drink','agua'],['comer','eat','arroz'],['vivir','live','en Madrid'],['trabajar','work','en una oficina'],['escribir','write','cartas'],['comprar','buy','fruta'],['aprender','learn','algo nuevo'],['abrir','open','la tienda'],['ser','be','estudiantes'],['estar','be','en casa'],['tener','have','tiempo libre'],['ir','go','al parque']];
const ENGLISH={
 hablar:['speak with friends','speaks with friends','spoke with friends'],estudiar:['study Spanish','studies Spanish','studied Spanish'],beber:['drink water','drinks water','drank water'],comer:['eat rice','eats rice','ate rice'],vivir:['live in Madrid','lives in Madrid','lived in Madrid'],trabajar:['work in an office','works in an office','worked in an office'],escribir:['write letters','writes letters','wrote letters'],comprar:['buy fruit','buys fruit','bought fruit'],aprender:['learn something new','learns something new','learned something new'],abrir:['open the shop','opens the shop','opened the shop'],tener:['have free time','has free time','had free time'],ir:['go to the park','goes to the park','went to the park']
};
function englishMeaning(c){const who=subjects[c.p][1],tense=c.tense;if(c.verb==='ser'){const p=c.p===3||c.p===4,thing=p?'students':'a student';return who+' '+(tense==='present'?(c.p===0?'am':c.p===2?'is':'are'):tense==='preterite'?(c.p>=3?'were':'was'):'used to be')+' '+thing}
if(c.verb==='estar')return who+' '+(tense==='present'?(c.p===0?'am':c.p===2?'is':'are'):tense==='preterite'?(c.p>=3?'were':'was'):'used to be')+' at home';
const parts=ENGLISH[c.verb];return who+' '+(tense==='present'?parts[c.p===2?1:0]:tense==='preterite'?parts[2]:'used to '+parts[0]);}
const tenseMeta={present:{label:'Everyday present',signal:'Usually / normally',prefix:'Normalmente',why:'Present describes a habit or something true now.'},preterite:{label:'Finished past',signal:'Yesterday / one completed event',prefix:'Ayer',why:'Preterite treats yesterday as a completed event.'},imperfect:{label:'Past habits',signal:'Used to / repeatedly in the past',prefix:'Antes',why:'Imperfect describes a past routine or background.'}};
const accents=s=>String(s||'').trim().toLocaleLowerCase('es').normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g,'');
function inflect(verb,tense,p){if(irregular[verb])return irregular[verb][tense][p];const fam=verb.slice(-2);return verb.slice(0,-2)+endings[tense][fam][p]}
function allCards(){const res=[];for(const [v,eng,tail] of verbs)for(const tense of ['present','preterite','imperfect'])for(let p=0;p<subjects.length;p++){
 let phrase=tail;if(v==='ser'){phrase=p===3||p===4?'estudiantes':'estudiante'}
 res.push({id:`${tense}:${v}:${p}`,verb:v,eng,tail:phrase,tense,p,expected:inflect(v,tense,p)});
 }return res}
const CARDS=allCards();const ITEMS=Object.fromEntries(CARDS.map(x=>[x.id,x]));
function verbIntro(){open();mode='verbs';const data=read(V),scores=Object.values(data.items||{});const independent=scores.filter(x=>x.independent>0).length;
 show(`<main class="v65-main"><span class="v65-kicker">SAY WHAT YOU MEAN</span><h1>Spanish verbs, without guessing.</h1><p class="v65-lead">Here's the difference: <strong>hablo</strong> = I speak now, <strong>hablé</strong> = I spoke yesterday, <strong>hablaba</strong> = I used to speak. Let's make those choices automatic.</p><div class="v65-verb-intro"><div><b>1 · Who?</b><span>Yo, tú, ella, nosotros, ellos.</span></div><div><b>2 · When?</b><span>Usually, yesterday, or a past habit.</span></div><div><b>3 · Which form?</b><span>Type it. Learn what changes.</span></div></div><p class="v65-smallprint">${independent} distinct conjugations recalled without help out of ${CARDS.length} available combinations. This records individual checks, not conversational fluency.</p><h2>Choose one place to start.</h2><div class="v65-verb-choices"><button data-v65-tense="present"><b>Present</b><small>“I speak” — the essentials</small></button><button data-v65-tense="preterite"><b>Preterite</b><small>“I spoke” — completed events</small></button><button data-v65-tense="imperfect"><b>Imperfect</b><small>“I used to speak” — habits</small></button><button data-v65-tense="mixed"><b>Mix them up</b><small>Practice all three tenses in one set</small></button></div><div class="v65-actions"><button class="v65-link" id="v65AllIdeas" type="button">← Back to the learning sessions</button></div></main>`);
 document.querySelectorAll('[data-v65-tense]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>startVerbs(b.dataset.v65Tense));document.getElementById('v65AllIdeas').onclick=intro;}
function chooseDeck(focus,forced){let pool=CARDS.filter(c=>focus==='mixed'||c.tense===focus);if(forced?.length)pool=forced.map(id=>ITEMS[id]).filter(Boolean);
 const data=read(V),seen=data.items||{};const scored=pool.map(c=>{const x=seen[c.id];return {c,score:x?((x.independent||0)>0?20:(x.missed||0)>0?-5:6):0,random:Math.random()}});
 scored.sort((a,b)=>(a.score-b.score)||(a.random-b.random));let out=[];const remaining=[...scored];while(out.length<8&&remaining.length){let i=remaining.findIndex(x=>!out.some(y=>y.verb===x.c.verb));if(i<0)i=0;out.push(remaining.splice(i,1)[0].c)}return out;}
function startVerbs(focus='present',forced=null){open();mode='verbs';quiz={focus,deck:chooseDeck(focus,forced),n:0,firstAttempt:true,help:false,wrong:false,revealed:false,results:[],retries:0};renderVerb()}
function verbHelp(c){const fam=c.verb.slice(-2),form=c.expected,meta=tenseMeta[c.tense];if(irregular[c.verb])return `<b>Frequent irregular verb</b><p>${esc(c.verb)} changes its form. For ${esc(subjects[c.p][1])} in ${esc(meta.label.toLowerCase())}, learn <b>${esc(form)}</b>.</p>`;
 return `<b>Build it piece by piece</b><p>${esc(c.verb)} → <b>${esc(c.verb.slice(0,-2))}</b> + <b>${esc(endings[c.tense][fam][c.p])}</b> = <b>${esc(form)}</b>.</p><p>${esc(meta.why)}</p>`}
function verbTable(tense){return `<details class="v65-verb-table" id="v65Table"><summary>Show the endings table (this makes your attempt assisted)</summary><div class="v65-scroll-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Subject</th><th>-ar</th><th>-er</th><th>-ir</th></tr></thead><tbody>${subjects.map((s,i)=>`<tr><th>${esc(s[0])}</th>${['ar','er','ir'].map(f=>`<td>${esc(endings[tense][f][i])}</td>`).join('')}</tr>`).join('')}</tbody></table></div><p>These endings are for regular verbs. Ser, ir, estar and tener have irregular forms in some tenses.</p></details>`}
function renderVerb(){if(!quiz)return;const q=quiz,c=q.deck[q.n];if(!c)return verbDone();q.firstAttempt=true;q.help=false;q.wrong=false;q.revealed=false;q.retries=0;const meta=tenseMeta[c.tense];const label=q.focus==='mixed'?'Mixed tense practice':meta.label;let sample=`${meta.prefix}, ${subjects[c.p][0]} ___ ${c.tail}.`;const english=`${englishMeaning(c)} · ${meta.signal}`;
 show(`<main class="v65-main v65-verb-room"><div class="v65-step"><button id="v65VerbBack" type="button" class="v65-link">← Other verb lessons</button><span>QUESTION ${q.n+1} OF ${q.deck.length}</span></div><span class="v65-kicker">${esc(label)}</span><h1>What goes in the blank?</h1><p class="v65-lead">${esc(english)}</p><div class="v65-prompt v65-spanish-phrase" lang="es">${esc(sample)}</div><div class="v65-verb-key"><span>INFINITIVE</span><strong>${esc(c.verb)}</strong><span>SUBJECT</span><strong>${esc(subjects[c.p][0])}</strong><span>TENSE</span><strong>${esc(meta.label)}</strong></div><label class="v65-label" for="v65VerbInput">TYPE THE CONJUGATED VERB</label><form id="v65VerbForm"><div class="v65-verb-inputrow"><input id="v65VerbInput" autocomplete="off" autocapitalize="off" spellcheck="false" placeholder="e.g., hablo" aria-describedby="v65VerbFeedback"><button class="v65-primary" id="v65VerbSubmit" type="submit">Check answer</button></div></form><div id="v65VerbFeedback" class="v65-feedback" role="status" hidden></div><div class="v65-help"><button id="v65VerbHint" type="button" class="v65-link">Help me form it</button><button id="v65VerbReveal" type="button" class="v65-link">Show the answer</button><button id="v65VerbSpeak" type="button" class="v65-link">Hear the correct sentence</button></div>${irregular[c.verb]?'':verbTable(c.tense)}<div class="v65-actions" id="v65VerbNextWrap" hidden><button id="v65VerbNext" class="v65-primary" type="button">${q.n===q.deck.length-1?'See what I learned':'Next sentence →'}</button></div><p class="v65-smallprint">Accents matter in spelling. If your only mistake is an accent, we'll show where it belongs rather than calling the whole idea wrong.</p></main>`);
 document.getElementById('v65VerbBack').onclick=verbIntro;document.getElementById('v65VerbForm').onsubmit=e=>{e.preventDefault();checkVerb()};document.getElementById('v65VerbHint').onclick=()=>{q.help=true;feedbackVerb('One small step',verbHelp(c),'hint')};document.getElementById('v65VerbReveal').onclick=()=>{q.revealed=true;q.help=true;finalVerb(false,true)};
 const tbl=document.getElementById('v65Table');if(tbl)tbl.addEventListener('toggle',()=>{if(tbl.open)q.help=true});document.getElementById('v65VerbNext').onclick=()=>{q.n++;renderVerb()};document.getElementById('v65VerbSpeak').onclick=()=>{q.help=true;if(!window.speechSynthesis||!window.SpeechSynthesisUtterance){feedbackVerb('Audio unavailable','Your browser does not offer speech synthesis.','hint');return}try{const utter=new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(sample.replace('___',c.expected));utter.lang='es-ES';utter.rate=.87;speechSynthesis.cancel();speechSynthesis.speak(utter)}catch{feedbackVerb('Audio unavailable','Speech synthesis did not start on this browser.','hint')}};
 document.getElementById('v65VerbInput').focus()}
function feedbackVerb(head,body,cls){const el=document.getElementById('v65VerbFeedback');if(!el)return;el.hidden=false;el.className='v65-feedback '+cls;el.innerHTML=`<strong>${esc(head)}</strong>${body.startsWith('<')?body:`<p>${esc(body)}</p>`}`}
function diagnose(c,value){const bare=accents(value);if(!bare)return 'Try typing one verb form, not the whole sentence.';if(bare===accents(c.expected))return `You have the right sound and verb form. The correct spelling is <b>${esc(c.expected)}</b> — notice the accent.`;
 if(bare===accents(c.verb))return `That's the infinitive (“to ${esc(c.eng)}”). Change its ending to tell us who does it and when.`;
 for(const tense of ['present','preterite','imperfect'])if(tense!==c.tense&&bare===accents(inflect(c.verb,tense,c.p)))return `That's the <b>${esc(tenseMeta[tense].label.toLowerCase())}</b> form. Here the cue points to <b>${esc(tenseMeta[c.tense].label.toLowerCase())}</b>.`;
 for(let i=0;i<5;i++)if(i!==c.p&&bare===accents(inflect(c.verb,c.tense,i)))return `That form fits <b>${esc(subjects[i][0])}</b>. This sentence is about <b>${esc(subjects[c.p][0])}</b>.`;
 return `Not quite. First decide whether this is a routine now, one completed event, or something that used to happen. Then match the subject ${esc(subjects[c.p][0])}.`}
function checkVerb(){if(!quiz)return;const c=quiz.deck[quiz.n],el=document.getElementById('v65VerbInput');if(!el||el.disabled)return;const attempt=el.value.trim().toLocaleLowerCase('es').replace(/[.!?\s]+$/,'').trim();if(!attempt){feedbackVerb('Have a go','Just type the missing verb, not the full sentence.','hint');return}if(attempt===c.expected)return finalVerb(!(quiz.help||quiz.wrong||quiz.revealed),false);
 quiz.wrong=true;quiz.retries++;feedbackVerb('Close — here’s the distinction',`<p>${diagnose(c,attempt)}</p><small>Try editing the answer, or ask for one small step.</small>`,'miss');}
function finalVerb(independent,revealed){if(!quiz)return;const c=quiz.deck[quiz.n],inp=document.getElementById('v65VerbInput');if(!inp||inp.disabled)return;inp.value=c.expected;inp.disabled=true;document.getElementById('v65VerbSubmit').disabled=true;document.getElementById('v65VerbNextWrap').hidden=false;document.getElementById('v65VerbHint').disabled=true;document.getElementById('v65VerbReveal').disabled=true;
 const text=(independent?'You wrote it correctly without help.':revealed?'Here is the complete form.':'You found the correct form after practice.')+' '+tenseMeta[c.tense].why;
 feedbackVerb(independent?'You got it.':revealed?'Here is how to build it.':'Now it makes sense.',`<p>${esc(text)}</p>${verbHelp(c)}`,'solved');const data=read(V);data.items=data.items||{};let r=data.items[c.id]||{attempts:0,independent:0,assisted:0,missed:0};r.attempts++;r.last=Date.now();if(independent)r.independent++;else r.assisted++;if(quiz.wrong)r.missed++;data.items[c.id]=r;data.lastTense=quiz.focus;write(V,data);quiz.results.push({id:c.id,independent});}
function verbDone(){const results=quiz.results,correct=results.filter(r=>r.independent).length,missed=results.filter(r=>!r.independent).map(r=>r.id);show(`<main class="v65-main v65-ending"><span class="v65-kicker">A SHORT, REAL PRACTICE SESSION</span><h1>${correct} of ${results.length} without help.</h1><p class="v65-lead">These are individual verb forms you typed correctly on the first try, not a claim that you've mastered a tense. Every assisted attempt helped you practice too.</p><div class="v65-recap"><b>What you practiced</b><span>${results.length} different conjugations</span><span>${missed.length?`${missed.length} worth revisiting`:'No immediate misses in this set'}</span></div><div class="v65-actions"><button id="v65NewVerb" class="v65-primary" type="button">Another set →</button>${missed.length?'<button id="v65RetryVerb" class="v65-secondary" type="button">Practice the tricky ones again</button>':''}<button id="v65VerbLeave" class="v65-link" type="button">That's enough for today</button></div></main>`);document.getElementById('v65NewVerb').onclick=()=>startVerbs(quiz.focus);const b=document.getElementById('v65RetryVerb');if(b)b.onclick=()=>startVerbs(quiz.focus,missed);document.getElementById('v65VerbLeave').onclick=close;}
function decorateHome(){const host=document.getElementById('v62HomeFeature');if(!host)return;if(host.dataset.v65Home==='yes'){const d=read(K),b=document.getElementById('v65Start');if(b){const resume=d.draft>0&&DISC.missions.some(m=>m.id===d.current);const label=resume?'Continue your learning session →':'Start learning →';if(b.textContent!==label){b.textContent=label;b.onclick=resume?()=>begin(d.current,true):intro;}}return;}const buttons=host.querySelector('.v62-home-buttons');if(!buttons)return;host.dataset.v65Home='yes';const original=[...buttons.children];const start=document.createElement('button');start.type='button';start.id='v65Start';start.textContent='Start learning →';start.onclick=intro;buttons.prepend(start);
 const verbs=document.createElement('button');verbs.id='v65Spanish';verbs.type='button';verbs.textContent='Practice Spanish verbs';verbs.onclick=verbIntro;buttons.append(verbs);
 const details=document.createElement('details');details.className='v65-more';details.innerHTML='<summary>Other ways to learn</summary><div class="v65-more-actions"></div>';buttons.append(details);original.forEach(b=>details.lastElementChild.append(b));
 const h=host.querySelector('h2'),p=host.querySelector('.v62-home-copy>p');if(h)h.textContent='Pick something. We’ll take it from there.';if(p)p.textContent='Try a short lesson. You’ll see an example, answer a question, and find out why it works.';
 const hero=host.parentElement?.querySelector('.v36-home-hero');if(hero){const originalPaths=document.createElement('details');originalPaths.className='v65-original-start';originalPaths.innerHTML='<summary>Browse the original beginner paths and review tools</summary>';originalPaths.append(hero);host.append(originalPaths)}
 const d=read(K);if(d.current&&DISC.missions.some(m=>m.id===d.current)&&d.draft>0){start.textContent='Continue your learning session →';start.onclick=()=>begin(d.current,true)}
}
let scheduled=false;function watch(){if(scheduled)return;scheduled=true;queueMicrotask(()=>{scheduled=false;try{decorateHome()}catch(e){console.warn('Study Table session entry unavailable',e)}})}
function init(){new MutationObserver(watch).observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});watch();}
window.studyTableV65={version:65,intro,begin,close,verbIntro,startVerbs,readSessions:()=>read(K),readVerbs:()=>read(V),CARDS,inflect,tenseMeta};
if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',init,{once:true});else init();
})();

</script>

const normalize=s=>String(s??'').normalize('NFC').toLocaleLowerCase('es').replace(/[¿¡!?.,;:«»"']/g,' ').replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim();
const strip=s=>normalize(s).normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g,'');

What can I get you?'),

Anything else?'),

 line('Te entiendo. ¿En qué estás trabajando?','Say you are working on a new project.','Estoy trabajando en un proyecto nuevo.',['Trabajo en un proyecto nuevo.','En un proyecto nuevo.'],'Estar + gerund describes what you are working on right now.','Estoy trabajando en…','Estoy…','I understand. What do you need help with?'),
 line('¡Qué interesante! ¿Necesitas ayuda?','Say you are fine for now, thanks.','Por ahora estoy bien, gracias.',['No por ahora, gracias.','Estoy bien por ahora, muchas gracias.','Gracias, de momento no.'],'Por ahora and de momento mean for now.','Use Por ahora…','Por ahora…','How interesting! Do you need help?')]},
{id:'future',title:'Talk about next month',where:'Discussing plans with a friend',npc:'Ana',level:'Stretch',description:'Use the near future naturally.',closing:'You described a concrete plan and asked about someone else’s.',turns:[
 line('¿Qué vas a hacer el próximo mes?','Say you are going to study more Spanish.','Voy a estudiar más español.',['El próximo mes voy a estudiar más español.','Pienso estudiar más español.'],'Voy a + infinitive is a common future expression; pienso + infinitive expresses intention.','Start Voy a…','Voy a…','What are you going to do next month?'),
 line('¡Genial! ¿Por qué?','Say because you want to speak with more people.','Porque quiero hablar con más personas.',['Porque quiero hablar con más gente.','Para poder hablar con más personas.'],'Porque answers why; para explains a purpose.','Start Porque quiero…','Porque…','Great! Why?'),
 line('¡Me encanta! ¿Quieres practicar conmigo?','Say yes, you would love to.','Sí, me encantaría.',['Sí, claro, me encantaría.','Me gustaría mucho.','Sí, quiero practicar contigo.'],'Me encantaría is a friendly conditional expression for I’d love to.','Try Sí, me…','Sí…','I love it! Do you want to practice with me?')]},
{id:'opinions',title:'Give an opinion with nuance',where:'Discussing a film after dinner',npc:'Sofía',level:'Stretch',description:'Disagree gently, give a reason, and recommend something.',closing:'You shared an opinion and kept the conversation friendly.',turns:[
 line('A mí me encantó la película. ¿Y a ti?','Say you liked it, but thought it was too long.','Me gustó, pero me pareció demasiado larga.',['Me gustó, aunque fue muy larga.','Me gustó, pero era demasiado larga.'],'Me gustó evaluates your experience. Pero introduces a contrast.','Start Me gustó, pero…','Me gustó…','I loved the film. How about you?'),
 line('¿De verdad? ¿Por qué lo dices?','Say because some scenes were slow.','Porque algunas escenas eran lentas.',['Porque había escenas muy lentas.','Algunas escenas me parecieron lentas.'],'Eran is imperfect when describing background characteristics of scenes.','Start Porque algunas…','Porque…','Really? Why do you say that?'),
 line('Entiendo. ¿Me recomiendas otra?','Say you recommend another film because it is shorter.','Te recomiendo otra película porque es más corta.',['Recomiendo otra porque es más corta.','Sí, hay una película más corta que te recomiendo.'],'Te recomiendo means I recommend to you; más corta means shorter.','Te recomiendo… porque…','Te recomiendo…','I understand. Would you recommend another?')]}
];
const byId=Object.fromEntries(SCENES.map(s=>[s.id,s]));
let scene=null,turn=0,attempts=0,help=false,choice=null,voice=false,review=false,reviewLine=-1,previous=null;
function read(){try{const d=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(STORE)||'{}');return d&&typeof d==='object'&&!Array.isArray(d)?d:{}}catch{return {}}}
function save(d){try{localStorage.setItem(STORE,JSON.stringify(d))}catch(e){console.warn('Could not save conversation progress',e)}}
function allRows(){const d=read();return d.lines||{}}
function selectDue(){const now=Date.now();return Object.entries(allRows()).filter(([k,v])=>v&&v.due<=now&&byId[k.split(':')[0]]).sort((a,b)=>a[1].due-b[1].due)}
function el(id){return document.getElementById(id)}
function shell(){let node=el('v66Layer');if(node)return node;node=document.createElement('div');node.id='v66Layer';node.innerHTML=`<section class="v65-shell v66-shell" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label="Spanish conversation practice" tabindex="-1"><header class="v65-bar"><span class="v65-brand">STUDY TABLE <small> / CONVERSATIONS</small></span><button type="button" id="v66Close" aria-label="Close conversations">✕</button></header><div id="v66Body"></div></section>`;document.body.append(node);el('v66Close').onclick=close;node.addEventListener('click',e=>{if(e.target===node)close()});node.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Escape'){e.preventDefault();close()}if(e.key!=='Tab')return;const a=[...node.querySelectorAll('button:not([disabled]),input:not([disabled]),textarea:not([disabled]),summary')].filter(x=>x.getClientRects().length&&!x.closest('[hidden]'));if(!a.length)return;if(e.shiftKey&&document.activeElement===a[0]){e.preventDefault();a[a.length-1].focus()}else if(!e.shiftKey&&document.activeElement===a[a.length-1]){e.preventDefault();a[0].focus()}});return node}
function open(){const n=shell();previous=document.activeElement;n.classList.add('open');document.body.classList.add('v66-freeze');n.querySelector('section').focus()}
function close(){const n=el('v66Layer');if(!n?.classList.contains('open'))return;n.classList.remove('open');document.body.classList.remove('v66-freeze');try{speechSynthesis.cancel()}catch{}scene=null;if(previous?.isConnected&&previous.getClientRects().length)previous.focus()}
function show(html){shell();el('v66Body').innerHTML=html;el('v66Layer').querySelector('section').scrollTop=0}
function intro(){open();scene=null;const data=read(),done=data.scenes||{},due=selectDue();const resume=data.draft&&byId[data.draft.scene]&&data.draft.turn<byId[data.draft.scene].turns.length?data.draft:null;
show(`<main class="v65-main v66-main"><span class="v65-kicker">SPANISH / USEFUL IN REAL LIFE</span><h1>You're in the conversation.</h1><p class="v65-lead">Someone says something. You reply in Spanish. If you get stuck, the coach helps you build a real response—not guess a multiple-choice answer.</p><div class="v66-banner"><div><strong>12 everyday scenes · 36 exchanges</strong><p>Say it, write it, understand it. Your own words matter more than the score.</p></div>${due.length?`<button class="v65-primary" id="v66Due">Review ${due.length} line${due.length===1?'':'s'} →</button>`:''}</div>${resume?`<button id="v66Resume" class="v65-resume">Continue: ${E(byId[resume.scene].title)} · Exchange ${resume.turn+1} →</button>`:''}<div class="v66-grid">${SCENES.map(s=>{const rec=done[s.id];return `<button type="button" data-v66-scene="${s.id}" class="v66-card"><span class="v66-level">${E(s.level)}${rec?.count?' · Practiced':''}</span><strong>${E(s.title)}</strong><small>${E(s.description)}</small><span class="v66-card-foot">${E(s.where)} →</span></button>`}).join('')}</div><p class="v65-smallprint">The offline coach recognizes prepared phrases and their listed alternatives. It cannot fully assess unrestricted Spanish. You can compare a different phrasing yourself without it being recorded as independently verified.</p></main>`);
document.querySelectorAll('[data-v66-scene]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>begin(b.dataset.v66Scene));if(resume)el('v66Resume').onclick=()=>begin(resume.scene,resume.turn);if(due.length)el('v66Due').onclick=()=>startDue()}
function begin(id,at=0){const s=byId[id];if(!s)return intro();open();scene=s;review=false;turn=Math.max(0,Math.min(s.turns.length-1,at));renderTurn()}
function persistDraft(){if(!scene||review)return;const d=read();d.draft={scene:scene.id,turn};save(d)}
function state(){attempts=0;help=false;choice=null;voice=false}
function speak(text){if(!window.speechSynthesis||!window.SpeechSynthesisUtterance){message('Audio is unavailable','Your browser does not provide speech synthesis. You can still read the line.','hint');return}try{speechSynthesis.cancel();const u=new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(text);u.lang='es-ES';u.rate=.86;speechSynthesis.speak(u)}catch{message('Audio unavailable','Speech did not start in this browser.','hint')}}
function renderTurn(){state();persistDraft();const l=scene.turns[review?reviewLine:turn];const label=review?'RECALL / ONE MORE TRY':`EXCHANGE ${turn+1} OF ${scene.turns.length}`;
show(`<main class="v65-main v66-main"><div class="v65-step"><button id="v66Back" type="button" class="v65-link">← All conversations</button><span>${label}</span></div><span class="v65-kicker">${E(scene.where)}</span><h1>${E(scene.title)}</h1><div class="v66-dialog"><div class="v66-speaker"><span class="v66-avatar">${E(scene.npc[0])}</span><div><b>${E(scene.npc)}</b><small>${E(l.translation)}</small></div></div><p lang="es">“${E(l.npc)}”</p><button id="v66Listen" class="v65-link" type="button">▶ Hear what they said</button></div><div class="v66-task"><small>YOUR TURN</small><p>${E(l.goal)}</p></div><form id="v66Form"><label class="v65-label" for="v66Input">YOUR REPLY IN SPANISH</label><textarea id="v66Input" rows="2" maxlength="280" placeholder="Write what you would actually say…" lang="es" spellcheck="true"></textarea><div class="v65-actions"><button type="submit" id="v66Check" class="v65-primary">Check this reply →</button><button type="button" id="v66Hint" class="v65-secondary">Help me start</button><button type="button" id="v66Why" class="v65-secondary">Explain the grammar</button><button type="button" id="v66Reveal" class="v65-link">Show an answer</button></div></form><div id="v66Feedback" class="v65-feedback" role="status" aria-live="polite" hidden></div><div id="v66Continue" class="v65-actions" hidden><button id="v66Next" type="button" class="v65-primary">${review?'Back to conversations':turn===scene.turns.length-1?'Finish this conversation':'Hear what happens next →'}</button></div><p class="v65-smallprint">No rush. Not recognized ≠ incorrect. Your answer can be perfectly natural even if the offline phrase matcher doesn't know it.</p></main>`);
el('v66Back').onclick=intro;el('v66Listen').onclick=()=>speak(l.npc);el('v66Form').onsubmit=e=>{e.preventDefault();assess()};el('v66Hint').onclick=()=>{help=true;message('A way into the sentence',`<p>${E(l.tip)}</p><p><b>Starter:</b> ${E(l.start)}</p>`,'hint')};el('v66Why').onclick=()=>{help=true;message('Why this works',`<p>${E(l.why)}</p><p>Try writing it yourself before opening the example.</p>`,'hint')};el('v66Reveal').onclick=()=>reveal();el('v66Next').onclick=next;el('v66Input').focus()}
function message(head,body,cls){const n=el('v66Feedback');if(!n)return;n.hidden=false;n.className='v65-feedback '+cls;n.innerHTML=`<strong>${E(head)}</strong>${body}`}
function recognize(l,raw){const variants=[l.model,...l.alternatives];const exact=variants.find(v=>normalize(v)===normalize(raw));if(exact)return {kind:'recognized',example:exact};const accent=variants.find(v=>strip(v)===strip(raw));if(accent)return {kind:'accent',example:accent};return {kind:'unknown',example:l.model}}
function assess(){if(!scene||choice)return;const input=el('v66Input');const value=input.value.trim();if(!value){message('Give it a try','<p>Even a few words are enough to start. You can ask for a smaller step.</p>','hint');return}attempts++;const l=scene.turns[review?reviewLine:turn],result=recognize(l,value);if(result.kind==='recognized'){choice={kind:'recognized',independent:attempts===1&&!help};record(choice);complete(`That works. ${choice.independent?'You produced a recognized phrase without help.':'You got there through practice.'}`,`<p><b>Your reply:</b> ${E(value)}</p><p>${E(l.why)}</p>`,true);return}
if(result.kind==='accent'){help=true;message('The words match. Check the accent.',`<p>You wrote <b>${E(value)}</b>. This common spelling uses <b>${E(result.example)}</b>.</p><p>Fix the accent and check again, or open the model.</p>`,'miss');return}
help=true;message('I’m not sure about that phrasing',`<p>It might be perfectly good Spanish. I can check some common answers, but not every natural alternative.</p><p>Try another way to say it, or <button type="button" class="v66-inline" id="v66ModelInline">compare with a worked reply</button>. Your attempt won't count as independently checked.</p>`,'hint');el('v66ModelInline').onclick=()=>reveal();}
function reveal(){if(!scene||choice)return;help=true;const l=scene.turns[review?reviewLine:turn];el('v66Input').value||='';message('One natural way to say it',`<div class="v66-model" lang="es">${E(l.model)}</div><p>${E(l.why)}</p><p>Other acceptable versions: ${l.alternatives.map(E).join(' · ')}</p><p>Maybe you said it another way. That's fine. This is a model to compare against, not a claim that your version was wrong.</p>`,'hint');el('v66Reveal').disabled=true;el('v66Hint').disabled=true;el('v66Why').disabled=true;el('v66Check').disabled=true;el('v66Input').disabled=true;const more=document.createElement('div');more.className='v66-reveal-finish';more.innerHTML='<button type="button" class="v65-secondary" id="v66Compared">I compared my answer — continue →</button>';el('v66Feedback').append(more);el('v66Compared').onclick=()=>{choice={kind:'compared',independent:false};record(choice);el('v66Continue').hidden=false;el('v66Compared').disabled=true};}
function complete(head,body,disable){message(head,body,'solved');if(disable){['v66Check','v66Input','v66Hint','v66Why','v66Reveal'].forEach(id=>{const n=el(id);if(n)n.disabled=true})}el('v66Continue').hidden=false}
function record(result){const k=`${scene.id}:${review?reviewLine:turn}`,d=read();d.lines=d.lines||{};const old=d.lines[k]||{tries:0,independent:0,assisted:0,streak:0};const r={...old};r.tries++;if(result.independent){r.independent++;r.streak=Math.min(5,r.streak+1);r.due=Date.now()+[0,DAY,3*DAY,7*DAY,14*DAY,30*DAY][r.streak]}else{r.assisted++;r.streak=0;r.due=Date.now()+DAY}r.last=Date.now();r.lastType=result.kind;d.lines[k]=r;if(!review){d.draft={scene:scene.id,turn:Math.min(scene.turns.length,turn+1)}}save(d)}
function next(){if(review){intro();return}if(turn+1<scene.turns.length){turn++;renderTurn()}else finish()}
function finish(){const d=read();d.scenes=d.scenes||{};const r=d.scenes[scene.id]||{count:0};r.count++;r.last=Date.now();d.scenes[scene.id]=r;delete d.draft;save(d);const n=scene.turns.length;const rows=Array.from({length:n},(_,i)=>d.lines[`${scene.id}:${i}`]);const independent=rows.filter(x=>x?.lastType==='recognized'&&x?.streak>0).length;show(`<main class="v65-main v66-main v66-end"><span class="v65-kicker">THE CONVERSATION ENDS</span><h1>${independent?'You kept the conversation going.':'You worked through the conversation.'}</h1><p class="v65-lead">${E(scene.closing)}</p><div class="v66-summary"><b>${E(scene.title)}</b><p>${independent} of ${n} lines were most recently recognized without help. The others were useful supported practice—not failed learning.</p></div><div class="v65-actions"><button id="v66Again" class="v65-primary">Replay this scene →</button><button id="v66Other" class="v65-secondary">Pick another conversation</button><button id="v66Leave" class="v65-link">Finish for now</button></div></main>`);el('v66Again').onclick=()=>begin(scene.id);el('v66Other').onclick=intro;el('v66Leave').onclick=close}
function startDue(){const due=selectDue();if(!due.length)return intro();const [id]=due[0];const [sceneId,lineId]=id.split(':');const s=byId[sceneId];if(!s)return intro();scene=s;review=true;reviewLine=Number(lineId);turn=reviewLine;renderTurn()}
function addEntries(){const ending=el('v65Body')?.querySelector('.v65-ending');if(ending&&!el('v66AfterVerbs')&&/conjugations|individual verb forms/i.test(ending.textContent||'')){const actions=ending.querySelector('.v65-actions');if(actions){const button=document.createElement('button');button.id='v66AfterVerbs';button.className='v65-secondary';button.type='button';button.textContent='Use these verbs in a conversation →';button.onclick=intro;actions.append(button)}}const buttons=el('v62HomeFeature')?.querySelector('.v62-home-buttons');if(buttons&&!el('v66Conversations')){const b=document.createElement('button');b.id='v66Conversations';b.type='button';b.textContent='Have a conversation in Spanish →';b.onclick=intro;const verbs=el('v65Spanish');if(verbs)verbs.before(b);else buttons.prepend(b)}const v65Entry=el('v65ToVerbs');if(v65Entry&&!el('v66FromV65')){const b=document.createElement('button');b.id='v66FromV65';b.type='button';b.className='v65-secondary';b.textContent='Try a Spanish conversation →';b.onclick=intro;v65Entry.after(b)}}
let waiting=false;function watch(){if(waiting)return;waiting=true;queueMicrotask(()=>{waiting=false;addEntries()})}
function init(){new MutationObserver(watch).observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});watch()}
window.studyTableV66={version:66,SCENES,intro,begin,close,recognize,read,selectDue,startDue,normalize,strip};if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',init,{once:true});else init();
})();

</script>

<script>
/* Study Table V67 — piano instrument studio: audio-first playing, transparent help, durable progress. */
(()=>{'use strict';
const STORE='studytable_v67_piano',DAY=86400000;
const W=['C4','D4','E4','F4','G4','A4','B4','C5'],B=['C#4','D#4','F#4','G#4','A#4'];
const KEYMAP={a:'C4',w:'C#4',s:'D4',e:'D#4',d:'E4',f:'F4',t:'F#4',g:'G4',y:'G#4',h:'A4',u:'A#4',j:'B4',k:'C5'};
const FREQ={C4:261.63,'C#4':277.18,D4:293.66,'D#4':311.13,E4:329.63,F4:349.23,'F#4':369.99,G4:392.0,'G#4':415.30,A4:440,'A#4':466.16,B4:493.88,C5:523.25};
const E=x=>String(x??'').replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
const task=(id,chapter,title,kind,notes,story,why,clue)=>({id,chapter,title,kind,notes,story,why,clue});
const MISSIONS=[
 task('homec',1,'Find middle C','find',['C4'],'The pair of black keys is your landmark. The white key immediately to its left is C.','Every C sits just to the left of two neighboring black keys. The black keys are the map.','Look for a pair of black keys. Tap the white key just to their left.'),
 task('rightd',1,'One step to D','find',['D4'],'Start at C. Slide one white key to the right: that is D.','Adjacent white keys are called steps. C → D is one step upward.','D is the white key between the two black keys.'),
 task('middlee',1,'The other side: E','find',['E4'],'Find the pair of black keys. The white key immediately to their right is E.','C–D–E surround the two-black-key group.','The pair of black keys ends just before E.'),
 task('freshf',1,'Meet F','find',['F4'],'After E, go one more white key to the right. You have found F.','F starts the next three-black-key group.','F is just to the left of three black keys.'),
 task('fifthg',1,'Find G','find',['G4'],'F is to the left of three black keys. G is the next white key to the right.','G sits between the first and second keys of a three-black-key group.','Count one white key right from F.'),
 task('anchorc',1,'Find high C','find',['C5'],'Keep walking right from middle C until the letter names repeat.','The high C sounds twice the frequency of middle C: an octave.','High C is the rightmost white key on this one-octave keyboard.'),
 task('two-step',2,'A little staircase','walk',['C4','D4','E4'],'Play C, D, E. Only one white key changes each time.','Moving by neighboring notes is called stepwise motion.','Find C beside the pair of black keys, then travel right twice.'),
 task('downstairs',2,'Walk back home','walk',['E4','D4','C4'],'Start at E. Move down to D, then land on C.','Descending means the pitch becomes lower as you travel left.','Each move is one white key to the left.'),
 task('question-answer',2,'A question and answer','walk',['C4','E4','D4','C4'],'Reach up to E, then walk home through D.','C → E skips D; E → D → C comes down by steps.','The second note is two white keys to the right of C.'),
 task('up-and-over',2,'A small leap','walk',['C4','G4','E4','C4'],'Start at C, leap to G, then come back through E and C.','A leap skips notes between the starting and ending pitches.','G sits between the first and second black keys of the group of three.'),
 task('ear-neighbor',3,'Hear which way it goes','ear',['C4','D4','C4'],'Listen first, then find the three notes. It rises and returns.','Your ear begins to hear movement, not only individual notes.','The middle note is the neighboring white key to the right.'),
 task('ear-fall',3,'Hear the descent','ear',['G4','F4','E4'],'Listen to a short falling phrase, then copy it.','A descending phrase travels toward lower frequencies.','Begin on G, then move two white keys left, one at a time.'),
 task('ear-skip',3,'A bigger sound','ear',['C4','E4','G4'],'Hear how the notes move by skips rather than single steps.','C–E–G make a C major triad, a simple chord played as separate notes.','Skip one white key between the notes.'),
 task('ear-return',3,'Where did it end?','ear',['G4','E4','D4','C4'],'Listen to a melody that ends back at home.','A return to C can make the phrase feel settled.','It falls from G, first skipping to E, then stepping down.'),
 task('mary',4,'Three notes, a real song','song',['E4','D4','C4','D4','E4','E4','E4'],'Play the opening of Mary Had a Little Lamb.','Three notes and a repeated ending are already enough for a familiar tune.','Start on E; walk down to C, then return to E.'),
 task('twinkle',4,'A melody you know','song',['C4','C4','G4','G4','A4','A4','G4'],'Play the opening of Twinkle, Twinkle, Little Star.','The larger leap C → G creates the memorable opening.','C twice, a leap up to G twice, then A twice, back to G.'),
 task('ode',4,'Beethoven in eight notes','song',['E4','E4','F4','G4','G4','F4','E4','D4'],'Play the first line of Ode to Joy.','Repetition and stepwise movement make melodies playable before you read a staff.','Start on E. Repeat E; walk up F–G, then head back down.'),
 task('frere',4,'The question repeats','song',['C4','D4','E4','C4','C4','D4','E4','C4'],'Play the opening of Frère Jacques twice.','Recognizing a repeated pattern halves what you need to remember.','C, D, E, C; then the exact same notes.'),
 task('lullaby',4,'Make it gentle','song',['C4','E4','G4','E4','D4','C4'],'Play a simple original lullaby.','Moving from a leap back into steps gives a phrase shape.','Hear the high point G before returning to C.'),
 task('sunrise',4,'A sunrise','song',['C4','D4','E4','G4','A4','G4','E4','C4'],'A small original melody rises brightly before finding its way home.','The peak at A adds tension; ending on C releases it.','C–D–E, skip up to G, touch A, then reverse course.'),
 task('dance',4,'An eight-note dance','song',['C4','E4','G4','E4','F4','A4','G4','C5'],'Play an original phrase with a surprising high ending.','Not every melody returns to its starting note; this one ends one octave higher.','First C–E–G–E. Then F–A–G–high C.'),
 task('finale',4,'Your first recital','song',['C4','E4','G4','C5','B4','A4','G4','E4','D4','C4'],'Ten notes make a complete phrase: climb to high C, then arrive home.',"You have practiced steps, leaps, landmarks, repeated notes, and finding home.",'First climb C–E–G–high C. The second half descends B–A–G–E–D–C.')
];
const BY=Object.fromEntries(MISSIONS.map(m=>[m.id,m]));
let current=null,position=0,help=false,misses=0,complete=false,mode='guided',audioCtx=null,playing=false,playIds=[],pastFocus=null,composed=[],lastPressAt=0,composerChanged=false;
const el=id=>document.getElementById(id);
function read(){try{const d=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(STORE)||'{}');return d&&typeof d==='object'&&!Array.isArray(d)?d:{}}catch{return {}}}
function save(d){try{localStorage.setItem(STORE,JSON.stringify(d))}catch{}}
function shell(){let n=el('v67Layer');if(n)return n;n=document.createElement('div');n.id='v67Layer';n.innerHTML=`<section class="v67-shell" role="dialog" aria-label="Playable piano studio" aria-modal="true" tabindex="-1"><header class="v67-head"><div class="v67-logo">STUDY TABLE <span>/ THE PIANO ROOM</span></div><button id="v67Close" aria-label="Close piano room" type="button">✕</button></header><div id="v67Body"></div></section>`;document.body.append(n);el('v67Close').onclick=close;n.addEventListener('click',e=>{if(e.target===n)close()});n.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Escape'){e.preventDefault();close()}});return n}
function open(){const n=shell();if(!n.classList.contains('open'))pastFocus=document.activeElement;n.classList.add('open');document.body.classList.add('v67-frozen');n.querySelector('section').focus()}
function close(){stopDemo();let n=el('v67Layer');if(!n?.classList.contains('open'))return;n.classList.remove('open');document.body.classList.remove('v67-frozen');current=null;if(pastFocus?.isConnected&&pastFocus.getClientRects().length)pastFocus.focus()}
function view(html){open();el('v67Body').innerHTML=html;el('v67Layer').querySelector('section').scrollTop=0}
function keys(){return `<div class="v67-keyboard" id="v67Keyboard" aria-label="One octave interactive piano keyboard" role="group">${W.map((note,i)=>`<button type="button" data-v67-note="${note}" class="v67-white" aria-label="Play ${note}" style="--col:${i}"><b class="v67-label">${note==='C5'?'C⁵':note[0]}</b><small>${'asdfghjk'[i]}</small></button>`).join('')}${B.map((note,i)=>`<button type="button" data-v67-note="${note}" class="v67-black" aria-label="Play ${note}" style="--left:${[12.5,25,50,62.5,75][i]}%"><small>${['w','e','t','y','u'][i]}</small></button>`).join('')}</div><p class="v67-keyhint">Tap a key, or use <b>A S D F G H J K</b> on a computer keyboard. Black keys: <b>W E T Y U</b>.</p>`}
function bindKeyboard(cb){el('v67Keyboard')?.querySelectorAll('[data-v67-note]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>cb(b.dataset.v67Note))}
function tone(note,when=null,duration=.35){let ctx;try{const Audio=window.AudioContext||window.webkitAudioContext;if(!Audio)return false;ctx=audioCtx&&audioCtx.state!=='closed'?audioCtx:(audioCtx=new Audio());if(ctx.state==='suspended')ctx.resume().catch(()=>{});const t=when??ctx.currentTime;const o=ctx.createOscillator(),g=ctx.createGain();o.type='triangle';o.frequency.setValueAtTime(FREQ[note],t);g.gain.setValueAtTime(.0001,t);g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.20,t+.018);g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.0001,t+Math.max(.10,duration));o.connect(g).connect(ctx.destination);o.start(t);o.stop(t+duration+.04);return true}catch{return false}}
function flash(note){const b=el('v67Keyboard')?.querySelector(`[data-v67-note="${note}"]`);if(!b)return;b.classList.add('v67-pressed');setTimeout(()=>{if(b.isConnected)b.classList.remove('v67-pressed')},170)}
function stopDemo(){playIds.forEach(clearTimeout);playIds=[];playing=false}
function demo(notes,light=false){if(playing||!notes.length)return;playing=true;
// Acquire the audio context during the actual click, before delayed playback begins.
try{const Audio=window.AudioContext||window.webkitAudioContext;if(Audio){audioCtx=audioCtx&&audioCtx.state!=='closed'?audioCtx:new Audio();if(audioCtx.state==='suspended')audioCtx.resume().catch(()=>{})}}catch{}
const step=440,notesCopy=notes.slice();notesCopy.forEach((n,i)=>{playIds.push(setTimeout(()=>{tone(n);if(light)flash(n)},i*step+80))});playIds.push(setTimeout(()=>{playing=false;playIds=[]},notes.length*step+200))}
function getStats(){let d=read();return d.missions||{}}
function getDue(){const now=Date.now(),r=getStats();return MISSIONS.filter(m=>r[m.id]?.due&&r[m.id].due<=now)}
function intro(){stopDemo();current=null;const records=getStats(),practiced=MISSIONS.filter(m=>records[m.id]?.tries).length,independent=MISSIONS.filter(m=>records[m.id]?.independent).length,due=getDue(),next=MISSIONS.find(m=>!records[m.id]?.tries)||MISSIONS[0];view(`<main class="v67-main"><span class="v67-eyebrow">PLAY FIRST. UNDERSTAND AS YOU GO.</span><h1>Make music in your first minute.</h1><p class="v67-lead">No sheet music required. Find your first key, train your ear, play little songs, then invent your own. Every note makes a sound.</p><div class="v67-feature"><div><strong>${E(next.title)}</strong><p>${E(next.story)}</p><div class="v67-sub">${practiced} of ${MISSIONS.length} lessons tried · ${independent} completed independently</div></div><button id="v67Start" class="v67-primary">${practiced?'Continue →':'Play my first note →'}</button></div><div class="v67-actions">${due.length?`<button id="v67Due" class="v67-secondary">Review ${due.length} older idea${due.length===1?'':'s'} →</button>`:''}<button id="v67Jam" class="v67-secondary">Make my own tune →</button><button id="v67Old" class="v67-quiet">Full piano curriculum</button></div><h2>Choose what sounds fun</h2><div class="v67-chapters">${[1,2,3,4].map((chapter,i)=>{const labels=['01 / FIND THE KEYS','02 / PLAY WITH BOTH DIRECTIONS','03 / TRAIN YOUR EAR','04 / PLAY REAL MELODIES'];return `<div class="v67-chapter"><span>${labels[i]}</span><div class="v67-lessons">${MISSIONS.filter(m=>m.chapter===chapter).map(m=>`<button type="button" data-v67-mission="${m.id}" class="v67-lesson"><b>${E(m.title)}</b><small>${records[m.id]?.independent?'Played independently':records[m.id]?.tries?'Practiced':'Not started'}</small></button>`).join('')}</div></div>`}).join('')}</div><p class="v67-fine">The browser plays simple synthesized tones. Timing and fingering aren't formally assessed, and you don't need a physical piano.</p></main>`);el('v67Start').onclick=()=>begin(next.id);if(due.length)el('v67Due').onclick=()=>begin(due[0].id,true);el('v67Jam').onclick=jam;el('v67Old').onclick=()=>{close();window.v35Open?.('piano',0)};el('v67Body').querySelectorAll('[data-v67-mission]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>begin(b.dataset.v67Mission))}
function begin(id,review=false){if(!BY[id])return intro();stopDemo();current={...BY[id],review};position=0;misses=0;help=false;complete=false;mode=current.kind==='ear'?'challenge':'guided';renderLesson()}
function marker(note){return `<span class="v67-notechip">${note.replace('4','').replace('5','⁵')}</span>`}
function renderLesson(){const m=current;if(!m)return intro();let idx=MISSIONS.findIndex(x=>x.id===m.id),guided=mode==='guided',ear=m.kind==='ear';view(`<main class="v67-main v67-playing"><div class="v67-backrow"><button id="v67Back" class="v67-quiet">← Play piano</button><span>LESSON ${idx+1} OF ${MISSIONS.length}${m.review?' · REVIEW':''}</span></div><span class="v67-eyebrow">${['','KNOW THE KEYBOARD','MOVE YOUR HAND','PLAY BY EAR','LET IT SOUND LIKE MUSIC'][m.chapter]}</span><h1>${E(m.title)}</h1><p class="v67-lead">${E(m.story)}</p><div class="v67-callout" id="v67Instruction">${m.kind==='find'?`<b>One goal:</b> Press <b>${m.notes[0].replace('4','').replace('5',' (high)')}</b> on the piano below.`:ear?'<b>Listen first.</b> Press “Hear the phrase,” then copy what you heard. The answer isn’t shown until you ask.':'<b>Play the phrase.</b> Press each note from left to right. You can hide the guide and play from memory.'}</div><div class="v67-sheet" id="v67Sheet" ${!guided?'hidden':''}><span>PLAY THESE NOTES</span><div>${m.notes.map(marker).join('')}</div></div><div class="v67-progress"><span id="v67StepText">${ear?'Ready to listen':`Note 1 of ${m.notes.length}`}</span><div class="v67-meter"><div id="v67MeterFill"></div></div></div><div class="v67-board ${guided?'v67-guided':'v67-solo'}" id="v67Board">${keys()}</div><div class="v67-actions"><button id="v67Hear" class="v67-secondary">▶ Hear the phrase</button><button id="v67Guide" class="v67-secondary">${guided?'Try without help':'Show the notes and keys'}</button><button id="v67Reset" class="v67-quiet">↻ Start again</button><button id="v67Clue" class="v67-quiet">Help me find it</button></div><div class="v67-feedback" id="v67Feedback" role="status" aria-live="polite">${ear?'Listen, then find the notes by ear.':guided?'The green key shows your next note.':'Play from memory. The keyboard won’t reveal your next note.'}</div><div class="v67-explain" id="v67Done" hidden></div><p class="v67-fine">A visual guide, clue, wrong note, or revealed answer means supported practice—not an independent completion. Hearing the phrase is part of ear training and doesn’t count as a hint.</p></main>`);el('v67Back').onclick=intro;el('v67Hear').onclick=()=>{demo(m.notes,false);el('v67Feedback').textContent='Listen for the shape of the phrase. Then try it on the keys.'};el('v67Guide').onclick=()=>{mode=mode==='guided'?'challenge':'guided';help=true;position=0;misses=0;complete=false;renderLesson()};el('v67Clue').onclick=()=>{help=true;el('v67Feedback').innerHTML=`<b>A smaller step:</b> ${E(m.clue)} <button type="button" class="v67-inline" id="v67Reveal">Show the whole sequence</button>`;el('v67Reveal').onclick=()=>{help=true;mode='guided';renderLesson();el('v67Feedback').textContent='The note sequence is shown above. Follow it slowly; this counts as supported practice.'}};el('v67Reset').onclick=()=>{position=0;misses=0;help=true;complete=false;paint();el('v67Done').hidden=true;el('v67Feedback').textContent='Start again. You can take it one note at a time.'};bindKeyboard(press);paint()}
function paint(){if(!current)return;const m=current,g=mode==='guided',target=m.notes[position];el('v67Board')?.classList.toggle('v67-guided',g);el('v67Board')?.classList.toggle('v67-solo',!g);el('v67Sheet')?.toggleAttribute('hidden',!g);el('v67Keyboard')?.querySelectorAll('[data-v67-note]').forEach(b=>b.classList.toggle('v67-target',g&&b.dataset.v67Note===target));const t=el('v67StepText');if(t)t.textContent=complete?'Phrase complete':`Note ${Math.min(position+1,m.notes.length)} of ${m.notes.length}`;const bar=el('v67MeterFill');if(bar)bar.style.width=`${Math.round(position/m.notes.length*100)}%`}
function describeMiss(pressed,wanted){const a=W.indexOf(pressed),b=W.indexOf(wanted);if(a<0)return `That was a black key. ${wanted} is a white key. Find the pattern of two or three black keys to orient yourself.`;if(a===b)return 'That sounds right; try again.';const delta=b-a;if(Math.abs(delta)===1)return `Close. Move one white key ${delta>0?'right (higher)':'left (lower)'}.`;return `That was ${pressed.replace('4','').replace('5',' high')}. The target is ${Math.abs(delta)} white keys ${delta>0?'to the right (higher)':'to the left (lower)'}.`}
function press(note){tone(note);flash(note);if(!current||playing||complete)return;const m=current,wanted=m.notes[position];if(note!==wanted){misses++;help=true;const feedback=el('v67Feedback');feedback.innerHTML=`<strong>Good exploration.</strong> ${E(describeMiss(note,wanted))}`;return}position++;paint();if(position<m.notes.length){el('v67Feedback').textContent=mode==='guided'?'Nice. The next green key is your next note.':'You found it. Keep going by ear or memory.';return}finish()}
function finish(){if(!current||complete)return;complete=true;const m=current,independent=mode==='challenge'&&!help&&!misses,d=read();d.missions=d.missions||{};let r=d.missions[m.id]||{tries:0,independent:0,assisted:0,streak:0};r.tries++;if(independent){r.independent++;r.streak=Math.min(5,r.streak+1);r.due=Date.now()+[0,DAY,3*DAY,7*DAY,14*DAY,30*DAY][r.streak]}else{r.assisted++;r.streak=0;r.due=Date.now()+DAY}r.last=Date.now();r.lastMode=independent?'independent':'supported';d.missions[m.id]=r;save(d);const next=MISSIONS[MISSIONS.findIndex(x=>x.id===m.id)+1];const done=el('v67Done');done.hidden=false;done.innerHTML=`<span>THAT WAS MUSIC</span><h2>${independent?'You played it without visible help.':'You played the whole phrase.'}</h2><p>${E(m.why)}</p><p class="v67-fine">${independent?'Recorded as a right on your first try keyboard sequence. Rhythm and fingering were not assessed.':'Recorded as supported practice. To test recall, choose “Play it from memory.”'}</p><div class="v67-actions"><button type="button" id="v67Replay" class="v67-secondary">${independent?'Play it again':'Play it from memory'}</button>${next?'<button type="button" id="v67Next" class="v67-primary">Next discovery →</button>':'<button type="button" id="v67JamNext" class="v67-primary">Make your own tune →</button>'}<button type="button" id="v67DoneHome" class="v67-quiet">All lessons</button></div>`;el('v67Feedback').textContent='You reached the final note. Hear how the phrase feels complete?';el('v67Replay').onclick=()=>{mode='challenge';position=0;misses=0;help=false;complete=false;renderLesson()};if(next)el('v67Next').onclick=()=>begin(next.id);else el('v67JamNext').onclick=jam;el('v67DoneHome').onclick=intro;paint()}
function jam(){stopDemo();current=null;const d=read();composed=(d.composition||[]).slice(0,32);composerChanged=false;view(`<main class="v67-main v67-jam"><div class="v67-backrow"><button id="v67JamBack" class="v67-quiet">← Play piano</button><span>YOUR OWN MUSIC</span></div><span class="v67-eyebrow">A LITTLE CREATIVE FREEDOM</span><h1>What if you wrote a song?</h1><p class="v67-lead">Tap any notes in any order. const patterns=[{name:'The steady pulse',detail:'Eight even quarter notes',hits:[0,1,2,3,4,5,6,7],hint:'Count 1, 2, 3, 4 twice. Tap every number.'},{name:'Leave some space',detail:'Four half notes',hits:[0,2,4,6],hint:'Tap on 1 and 3. Let the sound breathe between taps.'},{name:'The backbeat',detail:'Tap on beats 2 and 4',hits:[1,3,5,7],hint:'Count all four beats, but tap only on 2 and 4.'},{name:'A little syncopation',detail:'Some offbeat eighth notes',hits:[0,.5,1.5,2,3,4,4.5,5.5,6,7],hint:'Say “one and two and three and four and.” Half beats land on “and.”'},{name:'Short-short-long',detail:'Repeated rhythmic phrase',hits:[0,.5,1.5,2,2.5,3.5,4,4.5,5.5,6,6.5,7.5],hint:'Two close taps, then a longer gap. let choice=0,playing=false,beatMs=750,first=0,timer=null,audio=null,taps=[],scheduled=[],oldFocus=null;
function context(){try{audio=audio||new (window.AudioContext||window.webkitAudioContext)();if(audio.state==='suspended')audio.resume();return audio}catch(e){return null}}
function tick(accent=false){const a=context();if(!a)return;let o=a.createOscillator(),g=a.createGain();o.type='sine';o.frequency.value=accent?950:670;g.gain.setValueAtTime(.0001,a.currentTime);g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.16,a.currentTime+.004);g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.0001,a.currentTime+.085);o.connect(g).connect(a.destination);o.start();o.stop(a.currentTime+.09)}
function draw(){const p=patterns[choice];$('chooser').innerHTML=patterns.map((x,i)=>`<button class="choice ${i===choice?'active':''}" data-i="${i}"><strong>${i+1}. ${x.name}</strong><small>${x.detail}</small></button>`).join('');$('chooser').querySelectorAll('button').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{if(playing)stop();choice=Number(b.dataset.i);draw()});$('bar').innerHTML=Array.from({length:8},(_,i)=>`<div class="beat" id="beat${i}">${i%4+1}<em>${p.hits.some(x=>Math.floor(x)===i)?'● tap':'· wait'}</em></div>`).join('');$('hint').textContent=p.hint}
function highlight(i){for(let n=0;n<8;n++)$('beat'+n).classList.toggle('now',n===i)}

function evaluate(){
 const expected=patterns[choice].hits.map(x=>first+x*beatMs),used=new Set();let matched=0,errors=[];
 for(const t of taps){let best=-1,dist=Infinity;expected.forEach((e,i)=>{const d=Math.abs(t-e);if(!used.has(i)&&d<dist){best=i;dist=d}});if(best>=0&&dist<=Math.min(240,beatMs*.32)){used.add(best);matched++;errors.push(dist)}}
 const missed=expected.length-matched,extra=Math.max(0,taps.length-matched);
 const precision=errors.length?Math.round(errors.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)/errors.length):null;
 // Both missed notes and extra taps matter: perfect recall requires exact events.
 const score=Math.round(100*matched/Math.max(expected.length,taps.length,1));
 const independent=$('independent').checked,quiet=$('quiet').checked;
 const verdict=score>=90?'Beautifully steady.':score>=65?'You are finding the groove.':'Try a slower tempo and listen for the spaces.';
 $('result').textContent=`${verdict} ${score}% rhythm match. ${matched} on-time taps, ${missed} missed, ${extra} extra. ${precision===null?'No matched taps yet.':'Average timing difference: '+precision+' ms.'} ${independent?'Independent mode.':'Guided mode.'} ${quiet?'Count-in only.':'Metronome on.'}`;
 try{const key='studytable_v68_rhythm',old=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(key)||'{}');const entry=old[choice]||{};old[choice]={attempts:(entry.attempts||0)+1,best:Math.max(entry.best||0,score),bestIndependent:Math.max(entry.bestIndependent||0,independent?score:0),last:Date.now(),lastScore:score,lastIndependent:independent};localStorage.setItem(key,JSON.stringify(old));v69Status()}catch(e){}
}
function start(){stop();context();playing=true;taps=[];beatMs=60000/Number($('bpm').value);$('start').disabled=true;$('tap').disabled=true;$('result').textContent='Count in: 1, 2, 3, 4…';const startAt=performance.now()+130;first=startAt+4*beatMs;let last=-1;timer=setInterval(()=>{const now=performance.now(),step=Math.floor((now-startAt)/beatMs);if(step!==last&&step>=0&&step<12){last=step;if(step<4||!$('quiet').checked)tick(step%4===0);if(step<4){$('result').textContent=`Count in: ${step+1} / 4`;highlight(-1)}else{$('tap').disabled=false;v69Mask($('independent').checked);highlight(step-4);$('result').textContent=$('independent').checked?'Your turn. Keep the rhythm in your head.':'Your turn. Tap the marked beats.'}}if(now>=first+8*beatMs+170){stop();evaluate()}},20)}
function tap(){if(!playing||performance.now()<first-100||performance.now()>first+8*beatMs+100)return;taps.push(performance.now());tick(false);$('tap').style.filter='brightness(1.3)';setTimeout(()=>$('tap').style.filter='',80)}
$('launch').onclick=()=>{oldFocus=document.activeElement;$('shade').classList.add('open');$('close').focus();draw()};$('close').onclick=()=>{stop();$('shade').classList.remove('open');oldFocus?.focus?.()};$('shade').onclick=e=>{if(e.target===$('shade'))$('close').click()};$('start').onclick=start;$('stop').onclick=()=>{stop();$('result').textContent='Stopped. Press Count me in to restart.'};$('tap').onclick=tap;$('bpm').oninput=()=>$('bpmout').textContent=$('bpm').value;
function v69Status(){try{const d=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('studytable_v68_rhythm')||'{}');const attempts=Object.values(d).reduce((n,r)=>n+(r.attempts||0),0);$('v69Saved').textContent=attempts?`${attempts} attempts saved on this device`: 'Progress is saved on this device'}catch(e){$('v69Saved').textContent='Local progress unavailable'}}
$('v69Export').onclick=()=>{try{const data=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('studytable_v68_rhythm')||'{}');const blob=new Blob([JSON.stringify({format:'studytable-rhythm-v1',exportedAt:new Date().toISOString(),progress:data},null,2)],{type:'application/json'});const url=URL.createObjectURL(blob);const a=document.createElement('a');a.href=url;a.download='studytable-rhythm-progress.json';a.click();setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(url),1000)}catch(e){$('v69Saved').textContent='Export unavailable in this browser'}};
$('v69Import').onchange=async e=>{const file=e.target.files?.[0];if(!file)return;try{if(file.size>100000)throw Error('File too large');const obj=JSON.parse(await file.text());if(obj.format!=='studytable-rhythm-v1'||!obj.progress||typeof obj.progress!=='object'||Array.isArray(obj.progress))throw Error('Not a rhythm backup');const safe={};for(let i=0;i<patterns.length;i++){const r=obj.progress[i];if(!r)continue;const numeric=['attempts','best','bestIndependent','last','lastScore'];if(numeric.some(k=>r[k]!==undefined&&(!Number.isFinite(r[k])||r[k]<0)))throw Error('Invalid score');safe[i]={attempts:Math.min(100000,r.attempts||0),best:Math.min(100,r.best||0),bestIndependent:Math.min(100,r.bestIndependent||0),last:r.last||0,lastScore:Math.min(100,r.lastScore||0),lastIndependent:!!r.lastIndependent}}localStorage.setItem('studytable_v68_rhythm',JSON.stringify(safe));v69Status();$('result').textContent='Rhythm progress restored.'}catch(err){$('result').textContent='Could not import: '+err.message}finally{e.target.value=''}};
v69Status();

draw();
})();

function next(){const t=subject(),p=t.params;stage++;hint=false;shown=false;attempted=false;let v0=p[0].min+Math.floor(Math.random()*(Math.floor((p[0].max-p[0].min)/p[0].step)+1))*p[0].step,v1=p[1].min+Math.floor(Math.random()*(Math.floor((p[1].max-p[1].min)/p[1].step)+1))*p[1].step;const key=t.id;const used=seenPairs[key]||(seenPairs[key]=new Set());let pair=`${v0}:${v1}`,tries=0;while(used.has(pair)&&tries++<100){v0=p[0].min+Math.floor(Math.random()*(Math.floor((p[0].max-p[0].min)/p[0].step)+1))*p[0].step;v1=p[1].min+Math.floor(Math.random()*(Math.floor((p[1].max-p[1].min)/p[1].step)+1))*p[1].step;pair=`${v0}:${v1}`}used.add(pair);previous=[v0,v1];$('v70Question').textContent=t.question(v0,v1);$('v70Answer').value='';$('v70Check').disabled=false;$('v70Feedback').textContent='';$('v70Answer').focus()}

function check(){const field=$('v70Answer'),raw=field.value;if(!raw.trim()){feedback('Enter a number first, or use a smaller step.');return}const val=Number(raw),t=subject(),expected=t.calc(...previous);if(!Number.isFinite(val)){feedback('Please enter a finite number.');return}if(Math.abs(val-expected)<=Math.max(.01,Math.abs(expected)*.005)){const independent=!hint&&!shown&&!attempted;const rec=state[t.id]||{correct:0,independent:0,attempts:0};rec.correct++;rec.attempts++;if(independent)rec.independent++;state[t.id]=rec;save();feedback('Correct: '+numeric(expected)+' '+t.units+'. '+(independent?'Solved independently.':'Solved with practice or help.')+' '+t.explain(...previous));progress();$('v70Check').disabled=true;return}attempted=true;const direction=val>expected?'Your number is too high.':'Your number is too low.';feedback(direction+' Check the relationship, then try again. You can request a smaller step.')}
function progress(){const r=state[subject().id]||{};$('v70Progress').textContent=`Saved on this device: ${r.correct||0} correct problems, ${r.independent||0} solved on the first try without hints. This is practice evidence, not proof of mastery.`}
function plot(){
 const canvas=$('v70Graph'),ctx=canvas.getContext('2d'),w=canvas.width,h=canvas.height,t=subject(),a=values[0],b=values[1];
 const left=72,right=w-28,top=28,bottom=h-60;
 const spec=t.id==='calculus'?{xmin:-4,xmax:4,ymin:-8,ymax:80,xlabel:'x',ylabel:'y = '+a+'x²'}:
 t.id==='physics'?{xmin:0,xmax:5,ymin:0,ymax:110,xlabel:'time (s)',ylabel:'speed (m/s)'}:
 {xmin:0,xmax:20,ymin:0,ymax:180,xlabel:'price ($)',ylabel:'quantity'};
 const X=x=>left+(x-spec.xmin)/(spec.xmax-spec.xmin)*(right-left);
 const Y=y=>bottom-(y-spec.ymin)/(spec.ymax-spec.ymin)*(bottom-top);
 const clip=(v,lo,hi)=>Math.min(hi,Math.max(lo,v));
 ctx.clearRect(0,0,w,h);ctx.font='15px system-ui, sans-serif';ctx.textBaseline='middle';ctx.lineWidth=1;
 for(let i=0;i<=4;i++){
  const x=spec.xmin+(spec.xmax-spec.xmin)*i/4,y=spec.ymin+(spec.ymax-spec.ymin)*i/4;
  ctx.strokeStyle='#d8e7e3';ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(X(x),top);ctx.lineTo(X(x),bottom);ctx.moveTo(left,Y(y));ctx.lineTo(right,Y(y));ctx.stroke();
  ctx.fillStyle='#52645e';ctx.textAlign='center';ctx.fillText(numeric(x),X(x),bottom+19);
  ctx.textAlign='right';ctx.fillText(numeric(y),left-10,Y(y));
 }
 ctx.fillStyle='#263f38';ctx.textAlign='center';ctx.fillText(spec.xlabel,(left+right)/2,h-15);
 ctx.save();ctx.translate(18,(top+bottom)/2);ctx.rotate(-Math.PI/2);ctx.fillText(spec.ylabel,0,0);ctx.restore();
 ctx.save();ctx.beginPath();ctx.rect(left,top,right-left,bottom-top);ctx.clip();
 ctx.strokeStyle='#17665c';ctx.lineWidth=4;ctx.beginPath();
 if(t.id==='calculus'){
  for(let i=0;i<=200;i++){const x=-4+8*i/200,y=a*x*x;i?ctx.lineTo(X(x),Y(y)):ctx.moveTo(X(x),Y(y))}ctx.stroke();
  const x=b,y=a*x*x,m=2*a*x;ctx.strokeStyle='#d17b3f';ctx.lineWidth=3;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(X(-4),Y(y+m*(-4-x)));ctx.lineTo(X(4),Y(y+m*(4-x)));ctx.stroke();
  ctx.fillStyle='#d17b3f';ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(X(x),Y(y),7,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();
 }else if(t.id==='physics'){
  for(let i=0;i<=100;i++){const sec=i/20,speed=a/b*sec;i?ctx.lineTo(X(sec),Y(speed)):ctx.moveTo(X(sec),Y(speed))}ctx.stroke();
  ctx.fillStyle='#d17b3f';ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(X(5),Y(a/b*5),7,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();
 }else{
  for(let i=0;i<=100;i++){const price=i/5,quantity=Math.max(0,a-5*price);i?ctx.lineTo(X(price),Y(quantity)):ctx.moveTo(X(price),Y(quantity))}ctx.stroke();
  ctx.fillStyle='#d17b3f';ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(X(b),Y(Math.max(0,a-5*b)),8,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();
 }
 ctx.restore();
 $('v70GraphText').textContent=t.id==='calculus'?'Actual x and y axes. Orange tangent at x = '+b+'; its slope is '+numeric(2*a*b)+'.':
 t.id==='physics'?'Time versus speed, starting from rest. After 5 seconds: '+numeric(5*a/b)+' m/s.':'Price versus quantity demanded, using Q = max(0, '+a+' − 5P).';
}

})();

function parse(fen){let b={},r=8,c=0;for(const ch of fen.split(' ')[0]){if(ch==='/'){r--;c=0}else if(/[1-8]/.test(ch))c+=+ch;else{b['abcdefgh'[c]+r]=ch;c++}}return b}
function color(p){return p===p.toUpperCase()?'w':'b'}
function inside(f,r){return f>=0&&f<8&&r>=1&&r<=8}
function attacks(b,from,to){const p=b[from];if(!p)return false;let f='abcdefgh'.indexOf(from[0]),r=+from[1],tf='abcdefgh'.indexOf(to[0]),tr=+to[1],df=tf-f,dr=tr-r,a=Math.abs(df),z=Math.abs(dr),t=p.toLowerCase();if(!inside(tf,tr)||(b[to]&&color(b[to])===color(p)))return false;

if(t==='p')return dr===(color(p)==='w'?1:-1)&&(b[to]?a===1:df===0&&(dr===1||dr===-1));
if(t==='b'&&a!==z||t==='r'&&df!==0&&dr!==0||t==='q'&&!(a===z||df===0||dr===0))return false;
if(t!=='b'&&t!=='r'&&t!=='q')return false;
