function squareAttacked(b,s,by){const f='abcdefgh'.indexOf(s[0]),r=+s[1];for(const [from,p] of Object.entries(b)){if(color(p)!==by)continue;if(p.toLowerCase()==='p'){const ff='abcdefgh'.indexOf(from[0]),rr=+from[1];if(Math.abs(ff-f)===1&&r-rr===(by==='w'?1:-1))return true}else if(attacks(b,from,s))return true}return false}
function legal(b,from,to){const p=b[from];if(!p||color(p)!=='w'||!attacks(b,from,to))return false;const n={...b};delete n[from];n[to]=p;const k=Object.keys(n).find(s=>n[s]==='K');return !!k&&!squareAttacked(n,k,'b')}

function diagnostic(){const subject=$('v74subject')?.value||'spanish',question=$('v74body')?.querySelector('h3')?.textContent||'',selected=$('v74choices')?.querySelector('button.selected')?.textContent?.trim()||'',bank=window.studyTableV74?.bank?.[subject]||[],idx=bank.findIndex(q=>q[0]===question),q=bank[idx]||null;const specific=q?(misconception[subject]?.[idx]?.[selected]||q[4]):'Focus on what the question asks before eliminating the choices.';

function lab(){const tab=$('v70Room')?.querySelector('.v70Tabs [aria-pressed="true"]'),subject=(tab?.textContent||'Calculus').trim().split(' ')[0].toLowerCase(),question=$('v70Question')?.textContent||'',attempt=$('v70Answer')?.value||'',nums=question.match(/-?\d+(?:\.\d+)?/g)?.map(Number)||[];let step='',reason='',lead='';
if(subject==='calculus'){const a=nums[0],x=nums[1];step=`Differentiate ax² first: dy/dx = 2ax. Then substitute x = ${x}.`;reason=`2 × ${a} × ${x} = ${2*a*x}. This is the slope, not the y-value.`;lead=Number(attempt)===a*x*x?'You may have calculated the height of the curve instead of its slope.':'A derivative asks how steep the curve is at a point. Do not confuse y and dy/dx.'}
else if(subject==='physics'){const m=nums[0],f=nums[1];step='Start with F = ma, then isolate a = F ÷ m.';reason=`${f} N ÷ ${m} kg = ${+(f/m).toFixed(3)} m/s².`;lead=Number(attempt)===f*m?'Multiplying force and mass makes acceleration much too large. You need division.':'Check units: newtons divided by kilograms gives m/s².'}
else {const a=nums[0],p=nums[2]??nums[1];step=`Use Qd = ${a} − 5P. Substitute P = ${p} before subtracting.`;reason=`${a} − 5 × ${p} = ${Math.max(0,a-5*p)} units (with a zero floor in this toy model).`;lead='Apply multiplication before subtraction, and distinguish the demand intercept from quantity at a specific price.'}
return {subject,label:'Numerical problem',question,attempt,lead,step,reason,tinyIndex:0,assisted:()=>{$('v70Hint')?.click()}}}
function piano(){const title=$('v67Body')?.querySelector('h1')?.textContent||'Piano exercise',m=window.studyTableV67?.MISSIONS?.find(x=>x.title===title),stepText=$('v67StepText')?.textContent||'',pos=+(stepText.match(/Note\s+(\d+)/i)?.[1]||1)-1,target=m?.notes?.[Math.max(0,pos)],pressed=lastCaptured.piano?.note||'',direction=target&&pressed?window.studyTableV67?.describeMiss?.(pressed,target):'Orient yourself using groups of two and three black keys.';return {subject:'piano',label:'Keyboard practice',question:title,attempt:pressed?`Played ${pressed}`:'',lead:direction,step:'Start at C immediately to the left of two black keys. function watch(){for(const [id,cfg] of Object.entries(source)){const node=$(id);if(!node)continue;const t=extractText(node);if(!cfg.error(t))continue;let toolbar=node.querySelector(':scope > .v76-offer');if(toolbar&&toolbar.dataset.source===t)continue;if(toolbar)toolbar.remove();const info=cfg.read();toolbar=document.createElement('div');toolbar.className='v76-offer';toolbar.dataset.source=t;const b=document.createElement('button');b.type='button';b.textContent='Understand this mistake →';b.setAttribute('aria-label','Get help with this specific attempt');const muted=document.createElement('span');muted.textContent='Specific help, without leaving the lesson';toolbar.append(b,muted);node.append(toolbar);b.onclick=()=>{const old=node.querySelector(':scope > .v76-card');if(old){old.remove();b.textContent='Understand this mistake →';return}node.append(makeCard(cfg.read()));b.textContent='Hide coaching';record('opened',info.subject)}}}

const shade=document.createElement('div');shade.id='v77shade';shade.innerHTML='<section id="v77room" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label="Your next lesson" tabindex="-1"><header id="v77head"><div><span>STUDY TABLE · WHAT’S NEXT</span><h2>What to learn next</h2></div><button id="v77close" type="button" aria-label="Close lesson picker">Close ✕</button></header><main id="v77main"></main></section>';document.body.append(shade);
function show(){prevFocus=document.activeElement;shade.classList.add('open');document.body.style.overflow='hidden';phase='dashboard';render();el('v77room').scrollTop=0;el('v77room').focus()}
function close(){shade.classList.remove('open');document.body.style.overflow='';prevFocus?.focus?.()}
el('v77close').onclick=close;shade.onclick=e=>{if(e.target===shade)close()};document.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Escape'&&shade.classList.contains('open'))close()});
function launchHome(){let b=el('v77launch');if(b)return;const host=el('v62HomeFeature');const row=host?.querySelector('.v62-home-buttons');if(!row)return;b=document.createElement('button');b.id='v77launch';b.type='button';b.textContent='What should I study? →';b.onclick=show;row.before(b)}
// The original portal is mounted only on first navigation to the learning home.

homeObserver.observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});launchHome();
function subjectsMarkup(){return `<nav id="v77subjects" aria-label="Choose a subject">${Object.entries(S).map(([k,v])=>`<button type="button" data-subject="${k}" aria-pressed="${chosen===k}">${escape(v.label)}</button>`).join('')}</nav>`}
function bindSubjects(){el('v77subjects')?.querySelectorAll('[data-subject]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>chooseSubject(b.dataset.subject))}
function studio(){const route=S[chosen]?.route;close();if(route)route();else window.studyTableV65?.intro?.()}
function learning(id){close();window.studyTableV65?.begin?.(id)}
function quiz(id){selected=null;firstWrong=false;help=false;shown=false;answered=false;answerIndex=null;activeId=id;phase='question';render()}
function statusCounts(){const d=get();const rows=S[chosen].ids.map(id=>d.items[id]||{});return {due:rows.filter(r=>safeNumber(r.attempts)>0&&r.due<=NOW()).length,attempts:rows.reduce((sum,r)=>sum+safeNumber(r.attempts),0),clean:rows.reduce((sum,r)=>sum+safeNumber(r.clean),0)}}
function dashboard(){const best=topNodes(chosen),top=best[0],other=best[1],signals=practiceSignals(chosen),counts=statusCounts();const d=get();
 const extra=signals.recentMiss?`I noticed ${signals.recentMiss} recent correction${signals.recentMiss===1?'':'s'} across existing practice records. `:signals.examples?`I found ${signals.examples} existing practice event${signals.examples===1?'':'s'} in this subject. `:'You haven’t practiced this subject here yet.';
 el('v77main').innerHTML=`<p class="v77-sub">A place to start, based on what you’ve practiced so far.</p>${subjectsMarkup()}<section class="v77-feature"><span class="v77-pill">${escape(top.kind)}</span><span class="v77-eyebrow" style="display:block;margin-top:4px">UP NEXT</span><h3>${escape(missionById[top.id].title)}</h3><p class="v77-reason">${escape(top.why)}</p><div class="v77-actions"><button type="button" class="v77-primary" id="v77review">${top.kind==='New lesson'?'Start with an example':'See what I remember'} →</button><button type="button" class="v77-secondary" id="v77learn">${top.kind==='New lesson'?'Skip to a recall question':'Explain this from the beginning'}</button></div><p class="v77-quiet">Try answering without a hint. If you peek, you can always practice again.</p></section><div class="v77-columns"><section class="v77-tile"><span class="v77-eyebrow">THE OTHER IDEA</span><h4>${escape(missionById[other.id].title)}</h4><p class="v77-sub">${escape(other.why)}</p><button type="button" class="v77-plain" id="v77other">Try this instead →</button><div class="v77-statline">${safeNumber(other.row.attempts)?`${safeNumber(other.row.attempts)} recall attempt(s) · ${datePhrase(other.row.due)}`:'Not checked here yet'}</div></section><section class="v77-tile"><span class="v77-eyebrow">USE THE SKILL</span><h4>${escape(S[chosen].studio)}</h4><p class="v77-sub">Go straight to a hands-on activity. Don't just read about it.</p><button type="button" class="v77-plain" id="v77studio">Open the practice room →</button><div class="v77-statline">${escape(extra)}</div></section></div><div class="v77-mini"><span class="v77-statline">${counts.attempts} recall attempts · ${counts.clean} right on the first try · ${counts.due} ready to revisit in this subject</span><button class="v77-plain" type="button" id="v77all">See both review topics</button></div>`;
 bindSubjects();el('v77review').onclick=()=>top.kind==='New lesson'?learning(top.id):quiz(top.id);el('v77learn').onclick=()=>top.kind==='New lesson'?quiz(top.id):learning(top.id);el('v77other').onclick=()=>quiz(other.id);el('v77studio').onclick=studio;el('v77all').onclick=()=>{phase='overview';render()};}
function overview(){const all=S[chosen].ids.map(status);el('v77main').innerHTML=`<button class="v77-plain" id="v77back">← My next step</button><p class="v77-sub">Every subject has two introductory recall topics here. Deeper courses and practice rooms are still available through the original curriculum.</p>${subjectsMarkup()}<div class="v77-columns">${all.map(x=>`<section class="v77-tile"><span class="v77-pill">${escape(x.kind)}</span><h4>${escape(missionById[x.id].title)}</h4><p class="v77-sub">${escape(x.why)}</p><div class="v77-statline">${safeNumber(x.row.attempts)} attempts · ${safeNumber(x.row.clean)} first-try answers · ${safeNumber(x.row.attempts)?datePhrase(x.row.due):'Not scheduled'}</div><div class="v77-actions"><button class="v77-primary" data-quiz="${x.id}">Recall check</button><button class="v77-secondary" data-learn="${x.id}">Learn it</button></div></section>`).join('')}</div><p class="v77-quiet">If a subject already has due questions in the V63 review loop, that original loop is still accessible. Compass records are separate; successes are never added twice to V63.</p>`;bindSubjects();el('v77back').onclick=()=>{phase='dashboard';render()};el('v77main').querySelectorAll('[data-quiz]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>quiz(b.dataset.quiz));el('v77main').querySelectorAll('[data-learn]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>learning(b.dataset.learn))}
function cardFor(id){const d=get(),row=d.items[id]||{},index=safeNumber(row.attempts)%2;return questionBank[id]?.[index]||null}
function question(){const m=missionById[activeId],q=cardFor(activeId);if(!q){phase='dashboard';return render()}order=q.opts.map((_,i)=>i); // stable within this question; not reshuffled on selection
 const tr=safeNumber(get().items[activeId]?.attempts),d=get().items[activeId]||{};
 el('v77main').innerHTML=`<button type="button" class="v77-plain" id="v77back">← My next step</button><span class="v77-eyebrow" style="display:block;margin-top:14px">${escape(m.subject)} · CHECK WHAT STUCK</span><h3>${escape(m.title)}</h3><p class="v77-sub">Choose an answer before asking for help. If you get stuck, use the hint.</p><div class="v77-progress"><div style="width:${tr%2?75:40}%"></div></div><p style="font-weight:650;font-size:18px;line-height:1.5">${escape(q.ask)}</p><div class="v77-choices" id="v77choices">${order.map(i=>`<button type="button" class="v77-choice" data-choice="${i}" aria-pressed="false">${escape(q.opts[i])}</button>`).join('')}</div><div class="v77-actions"><button type="button" class="v77-primary" id="v77check" disabled>Check my answer</button><button type="button" class="v77-secondary" id="v77hint">A smaller hint</button><button type="button" class="v77-secondary" id="v77reveal">Show the explanation</button></div><div id="v77feedback" aria-live="polite"></div><p class="v77-quiet">Choose an answer to see the explanation. Written answers are checked only where an exercise supports them.</p>`;
 el('v77back').onclick=()=>{phase='dashboard';render()};el('v77choices').querySelectorAll('button').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{if(answered)return;selected=Number(b.dataset.choice);el('v77choices').querySelectorAll('button').forEach(x=>x.setAttribute('aria-pressed',x===b?'true':'false'));el('v77check').disabled=false});el('v77hint').onclick=()=>{if(answered)return;help=true;el('v77feedback').innerHTML=`<div class="v77-feedback"><b>Notice this:</b> ${escape(q.hint||'Start with the basic relationship and eliminate the choices that contradict it.')}</div>`};el('v77reveal').onclick=()=>finish(q,true);el('v77check').onclick=()=>{if(selected===null||answered)return;if(selected===q.correct)finish(q,false);else{firstWrong=true;help=true;el('v77feedback').innerHTML=`<div class="v77-feedback"><b>Not quite.</b> ${escape(Array.isArray(q.why)?q.why[selected]:q.why)}<p>Try choosing again, or show the full explanation. This one counts as practice with help. Try again later from memory.</p></div>`;el('v77choices').querySelectorAll('button').forEach(b=>{b.setAttribute('aria-pressed','false')});selected=null;el('v77check').disabled=true}};}
function finish(q,reveal){if(answered)return;answered=true;shown=reveal;const independent=!reveal&&!firstWrong&&!help&&selected===q.correct;
 const d=get(),r=d.items[activeId]||{attempts:0,clean:0,assisted:0,errors:0,streak:0};r.attempts=safeNumber(r.attempts)+1;if(independent){r.clean=safeNumber(r.clean)+1;r.streak=Math.min(5,safeNumber(r.streak)+1);r.due=NOW()+[0,DAY,3*DAY,7*DAY,14*DAY,30*DAY][r.streak]}else{r.assisted=safeNumber(r.assisted)+1;r.streak=0;r.due=NOW()+4*HOUR;if(firstWrong)r.errors=safeNumber(r.errors)+1}r.last=NOW();r.lastIndependent=independent;r.lastVariant=(r.attempts-1)%2;d.items[activeId]=r;d.events.push({id:activeId,at:r.last,independent,assisted:!independent,wrong:firstWrong,revealed:reveal});d.events=d.events.slice(-150);const saved=save(d);
 el('v77choices').querySelectorAll('button').forEach(b=>{b.disabled=true;if(+b.dataset.choice===q.correct)b.classList.add('v77-correct')});el('v77check').disabled=true;el('v77hint').disabled=true;el('v77reveal').disabled=true;
 const expected=q.opts[q.correct];el('v77feedback').innerHTML=`<div class="v77-feedback"><span class="v77-eyebrow">${independent?'RIGHT ON THE FIRST TRY':reveal?'WORKED EXPLANATION':'SOLVED WITH HELP'}</span><p><strong>${escape(expected)}</strong></p><p>${escape(Array.isArray(q.why)?q.why[q.correct]:q.why)}</p><p class="v77-statline">${independent?'Correct on the first try.':'You used some help with this one.'} ${saved?`Next check: ${datePhrase(r.due)}.`:'Progress could not be saved in this browser.'}</p><div class="v77-actions"><button type="button" class="v77-primary" id="v77next">Try another question →</button><button type="button" class="v77-secondary" id="v77learn">Learn the full lesson</button><button type="button" class="v77-secondary" id="v77done">Back to my session</button></div></div>`;
 el('v77next').onclick=()=>quiz(S[chosen].ids.find(x=>x!==activeId)||activeId);el('v77learn').onclick=()=>learning(activeId);el('v77done').onclick=()=>{phase='dashboard';render()};}
function render(){if(!shade.classList.contains('open'))return;if(phase==='question')question();else if(phase==='overview')overview();else dashboard();}
// Entry is available by API even when the user is viewing a course instead of the home screen.
window.studyTableV77={open:show,close,get,subjects:Object.keys(S),status,topNodes,practiceSignals,quiz,version:77};
})();

</script>

<script id="v78-coach-script">
/* Study Table V78 — Get help.
 * Zero-setup: deterministic, source-grounded, question-aware tutoring across eight subjects.
 * Optional: genuine open-ended inference via a local Ollama model and the bundled Python same-origin server.
 * Never send data to a remote AI provider and never imply that a free-text answer was graded if it wasn't.
 */
(()=>{'use strict';
const KEY='studytable_v78_coach',MAX_MSG=60,MAX_CTX=900;
const NAMES={spanish:'Spanish',piano:'Piano',chess:'Chess',medicine:'Medicine',economics:'Economics',physics:'Physics',calculus:'Calculus',history:'History'};
const IDS={spanish:['es-present','es-past'],piano:['pi-keys','pi-music'],chess:['ch-threat','ch-center'],medicine:['med-pressure','med-oxygen'],economics:['ec-trade','ec-incentive'],physics:['phy-force','phy-energy'],calculus:['calc-slope','calc-area'],history:['hi-sources','hi-cause']};
const GUIDE={
'es-present':{keys:['conjugat','present','hablo','hablar','tomo','ending','verb','yo','tú','usted'],micro:'Spanish changes the verb to match the person. “Hablo” already means “I speak”; “hablas” means “you speak.”',steps:['Start with the infinitive: hablar (to speak).','Remove -ar to find the stem: habl-.','For “I” in the present, add -o: hablo. For “you” (tú), add -as: hablas.','Use that form in a real sentence: Hablo un poco de español.'],analogy:'English adds an -s to “he speaks.” Spanish changes endings for more subjects, so the verb carries information that English often puts in a separate pronoun.',trap:'“Yo hablar” uses the dictionary form; “hablas” changes who is doing the speaking.',prompt:'Say “I work here” in Spanish. The verb is trabajar.',answer:'Trabajo aquí.',compare:'The -o ending indicates the person speaking. Hablas means you speak, not I speak.'},
'es-past':{keys:['past','preterite','imperfect','ayer','fui','iba','habits','yesterday','childhood','comí','comía'],micro:'The preterite shows an event as finished; the imperfect shows a habit, ongoing background, or scene in the past.',steps:['Ask what the sentence is doing: reporting an event or describing how things used to be.','One completed event: Ayer fui al parque (yesterday I went).','A repeated childhood habit: De niño iba al parque los sábados.','The same person can use both: Ayer fui, pero antes iba cada semana.'],analogy:'Think of a photograph (a completed event) versus the moving background of a scene (what life was like). This is a helpful starting analogy, not a universal rule.',trap:'“Yesterday” alone does not mechanically force the preterite; aspect and context matter. Habitual scenes typically use imperfect.',prompt:'Which past tense fits “As a child, I played piano every afternoon”: preterite or imperfect?',answer:'Imperfect.',compare:'Fui = a bounded trip; iba = a repeated trip or background action. The point is how you present the past event.'},
'pi-keys':{keys:['keyboard','key','middle c','black keys','two black','white key','octave','c d e'],micro:'The black keys repeat in groups of two and three. The white key immediately left of the two-black-key group is C.',steps:['Find any pair of black keys.','Move to the white key immediately to the left: C.','Moving right on white keys gives C, D, E, F, G, A, B, C.','Try finding a second C farther right: the pattern repeats.'],analogy:'The two-black-key group is a street sign. Once you can spot it, you can orient yourself on any part of the keyboard.',trap:'The white key between the two black keys is D, not C.',prompt:'What is the white key between the two black keys?',answer:'D.',compare:'Keyboard position matters more than remembering the total number of keys.'},
'pi-music':{keys:['melody','rhythm','pitch','home note','resolution','tempo','tune','beat'],micro:'Pitch tells you which note; rhythm tells you when it happens. In many C-major melodies, ending on C can feel like coming home.',steps:['Play C–D–E–D without stopping. Notice the open feeling.','Add C at the end and listen to the arrival.','Keep the exact notes, but vary the spacing. That changes rhythm, not pitch.','Count a steady pulse while playing a short phrase.'],analogy:'Pitch is the choice of words in a sentence; rhythm is its pacing and punctuation.',trap:'Playing the correct keys at wildly different times does not reproduce the melody accurately.',prompt:'If the notes are right but the melody sounds rushed, what should you practice?',answer:'Rhythm or a steady beat.',compare:'Changing the final volume alone does not fix inconsistent beat spacing.'},
'ch-threat':{keys:['threat','capture','hanging','blunder','defend','defended','free queen','attack','check'],micro:'Before taking a piece, ask what your opponent can do immediately afterward. Checks, captures and threats often matter more than the tempting prize.',steps:['Notice the attractive capture.','Ask what attacks the destination square after your piece lands there.','Look for the opponent’s checks and forcing captures.','Only then compare the material and choose the move.'],analogy:'A free-looking piece can be bait; you check the exit route before you reach for it.',trap:'Legal is not the same as safe. A legal capture can lose your queen or allow checkmate.',prompt:'Name the three forcing ideas worth checking before a tempting capture.',answer:'Checks, captures, threats.',compare:'A defended square can be unsafe for a valuable piece even when capture is legal.'},
'ch-center':{keys:['center','opening','develop','knight','bishop','pawn','e4','d4','control'],micro:'The central squares help pieces reach more of the board. Develop pieces while keeping your king safe instead of repeatedly moving the same piece.',steps:['Look at e4, d4, e5 and d5—the central squares.','Notice how a centralized knight can attack more squares than a corner knight.','Develop knights and bishops toward useful activity.','Before each move, still check immediate tactical threats.'],analogy:'The center is like a hub with many routes; a piece trapped in a corner has fewer choices.',trap:'Occupying the center at any cost is not the rule; development, safety, and concrete tactics still count.',prompt:'Why is a knight usually more active near the center?',answer:'It controls more squares.',compare:'A piece’s activity depends on where it can go and what it controls, not just how far forward it stands.'},
'med-pressure':{keys:['blood pressure','heart','resistance','cardiac output','artery','pressure','shock'],micro:'Arterial pressure depends substantially on blood flow from the heart and the resistance of blood vessels. A useful simplified relation is MAP ≈ cardiac output × systemic vascular resistance.',steps:['Ask what is pushing blood: cardiac output.','Ask how much the vessels resist that flow: systemic vascular resistance.','Lower output or lower vascular resistance can lower pressure.','Clinically, identify the cause and the patient’s condition rather than assuming every low reading means the same problem.'],analogy:'Imagine water flowing through pipes: the pump and the resistance both matter. Human circulation has more moving parts than a garden hose.',trap:'Blood pressure alone is not a diagnosis. This lesson is educational and not treatment advice.',prompt:'Name two major factors that determine mean arterial pressure in the simplified model.',answer:'Cardiac output and vascular resistance.',compare:'MAP ≈ CO × SVR is a simplifying relationship, not a complete physiological model.'},
'med-oxygen':{keys:['oxygen','breath','lungs','saturation','delivery','hemoglobin','hypoxia','respiration'],micro:'Getting oxygen to cells requires both oxygen entering the blood and enough blood flow to deliver it. 'ec-trade':{keys:['opportunity','tradeoff','scarcity','cost','choice','sacrifice','value'],micro:'Opportunity cost is the value of the best alternative you give up, not the sum of every possible alternative.',steps:['List the realistic choices available.','Choose the option you actually prefer.','Identify the single best option you would otherwise choose.','Its value is the opportunity cost of your decision.'],analogy:'If you spend an evening learning piano, you cannot spend that exact hour reading or working. The best alternative forgone is the cost.',trap:'Do not add every rejected option together; only the next-best alternative counts.',prompt:'If you choose A over B and C, and B is your best alternative, what is your opportunity cost?',answer:'The value of B.',compare:'The opportunity cost is defined by the next-best forgone use of the resource.'},
'ec-incentive':{keys:['incentive','supply','demand','price','quantity','market','elasticity','consumer'],micro:'In a simple demand model, a higher price reduces quantity demanded, all else equal. 'phy-force':{keys:['newton','force','mass','acceleration','f=ma','motion','speed','velocity'],micro:'Newton’s second law says net force = mass × acceleration. function noteMistake(a,choice){const key=exposureKey(a.topic,a.variant);let m=S.misses.find(x=>x.key===key);if(!m){m={key,topic:a.topic,variant:a.variant,times:0,wrongChoice:choice,resolved:false,lastWrong:0};S.misses.push(m)}m.lastWrong=Date.now();m.wrongChoice=choice;m.times=(Number(m.times)||0)+1;m.resolved=false;S.misses=S.misses.slice(-60);expose(a.topic,a.variant)}

function answer(index,alreadyLogged=false){const a=S.active;if(!a||a.done)return;index=Number(index);if(!Number.isInteger(index)||index<0||index>=a.q.opts.length)return;if(!alreadyLogged)you(`${String.fromCharCode(65+index)} · ${a.q.opts[index]}`);if(index!==a.q.correct){a.tries++;setLastQuiz(a,index);noteMistake(a,index);record('errors',a.topic);const details=Array.isArray(a.q.why)?a.q.why[index]:a.q.why;const msg=`Not quite. ${details||'That choice does not fit the situation.'}\n\n${a.tries>=2?'Here is a clue: '+(a.q.hint||guide(a.topic).steps[0]):'Consider why the other choices are wrong.'}`;say(msg,a.q.opts.map((opt,i)=>({text:`${String.fromCharCode(65+i)} · ${opt}`,action:()=>answer(i)})).concat([{text:'Why was that wrong?',action:()=>explainChoice(index)},{text:'Show the answer',action:()=>reveal()}]));return}

function matchTopic(text){const t=clean(text);if(t.length<3)return null;const words=t.split(/\s+/).filter(w=>w.length>2);let best=null,bestScore=0;for(const [id,entry] of Object.entries(GUIDE)){let n=id===S.topic?0.25:0;for(const k of entry.keys){const term=clean(k);if(t.includes(term)){n+=term.includes(' ')?4:2;if(id===S.topic)n+=.2}}if(n>bestScore){bestScore=n;best=id}}return bestScore>=2?best:null}
function localMath(t){let m=t.match(/(?:derivative|slope|differentiat).{0,55}?([+-]?\d+(?:\.\d+)?)?\s*\*?\s*x\s*(?:\^|\*\*)\s*2.{0,40}?(?:at|when)\s*x\s*=\s*([+-]?\d+(?:\.\d+)?)/i);if(m){const a=Number(m[1]||1),x=Number(m[2]);if(Math.abs(a)>1e6||Math.abs(x)>1e6)return null;const y=2*a*x;return `For f(x) = ${a}x², use the power rule: f′(x) = ${2*a}x. At x = ${x}, the slope is ${y}. This is the slope, not the value of the function.`}m=t.match(/(?:force\s*(?:is|=)?\s*)([+-]?\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*(?:n(?:ewtons?)?)?.{0,40}?(?:mass\s*(?:is|=)?\s*)(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*(?:kg)?/i);if(m){const f=Number(m[1]),mass=Number(m[2]);if(mass>0&&mass<1e6&&Math.abs(f)<1e6){return `Using a = F_net / m, a ${f} N net force on ${mass} kg gives ${Number((f/mass).toFixed(5))} m/s². This assumes the force given is the net force.`}}return null}
function interpret(s){const t=clean(s);if(!t)return;you(s);if(S.mode==='local'){askLocal(s);return}
 if(S.lastQuiz&&(/\bwhy (?:was|is) (?:option |choice |answer )?[abc]\b/.test(t)||/\bwhy (?:was|is) (?:that|my answer) wrong\b|\bexplain (?:that|my) (?:mistake|choice|answer)\b|\bwhat did i miss\b/.test(t))){const explicit=t.match(/\b(?:option|choice|answer) ([abc])\b/);explainChoice(explicit?explicit[1].charCodeAt(0)-97:null);return}

 if(/what i missed|review my mistakes|review mistakes|what did i get wrong|what should i review/.test(t)){reviewMistakes();return}
 if(/\bhint\b|small step|one step|first step/.test(t)){hint();return}
 if(/example|show me how|work(ed)? (problem|example)|use it in a sentence/.test(t)){example();return}
 if(/harder|more advanced|go deeper|challenge me/.test(t)){steps();quiz();return}

 const calculation=localMath(t);if(calculation){say(calculation,[{text:'Test me on this',action:quiz},{text:'Walk through it',action:steps}]);return}
 const known=matchTopic(t);if(known&&known!==S.topic){chooseTopic(known);const g=guide();say(`${g.micro}\n\n${g.compare}`, [{text:'Another example',action:example},{text:'Test me',action:quiz}]);return}

const plain=s=>normalize(String(s).normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g,'').replace(/ñ/g,'n'));

function evaluate(task,value){const input=String(value).trim();if(!input)return {status:'empty'};
 if(task.kind==='number'){
  const m=input.match(/^\s*(\$\s*)?([+-]?(?:\d+(?:\.\d*)?|\.\d+))\s*(.*?)\s*$/i);
  if(!m)return {status:'unverified'};
  const allowed={'dollars':['$','dollar','dollars','usd'],'units':['unit','units'],'meters':['m','meter','meters'],'m/s²':['m/s²','m/s^2','m/s2','meters per second squared'],'joules':['j','joule','joules'],'slope':[''],'square units':['square units','units²','units^2']};
  const suffix=m[3].toLowerCase().trim();
  if((m[1]||'')&&task.unit!=='dollars')return {status:'wrong',detail:`The number might be right, but this question needs ${task.unit}, not dollars.`};
  const accepted=allowed[task.unit]||[];
  if(suffix&&!accepted.includes(suffix)){
    const known=Object.values(allowed).flat().includes(suffix);
    return known?{status:'wrong',detail:`Check the units. This answer should be in ${task.unit}.`}:{status:'unverified'};
  }
  const n=Number(m[2]);return {status:Math.abs(n-task.answer)<1e-7?'correct':'wrong',detail:`${n} isn't the result. Try the first step rather than guessing.`};
 }
 const inputN=normalize(input);const valid=task.answers.some(x=>normalize(x)===inputN);
 if(valid)return {status:'correct'};
 if(task.kind==='phrase'&&task.answers.some(x=>plain(x)===plain(input)))return {status:'near',detail:'You have the right words. Check the accent marks and ñ before trying again.'};
 const recognized=(task.recognize||[]).some(x=>normalize(x)===inputN);
 if(recognized)return {status:'wrong',detail:task.note};
 if(task.kind==='note'&&/^[a-g]$/i.test(input))return {status:'wrong',detail:task.note};
 if(task.kind==='square'&&/^[a-h][1-8]$/i.test(input))return {status:'wrong',detail:task.note};
 return {status:'unverified'};
}
function progress(task){if(!data.progress[task.id])data.progress[task.id]={attempts:0,firstTry:0,assisted:0,errors:0,lastAt:0};return data.progress[task.id]}
function choices(ids){return ids.map(id=>getItem(id)).filter(Boolean)}
function recommend(subject){const options=ITEMS.filter(t=>t.subject===subject);const now=Date.now();
 const pending=options.filter(t=>{const p=data.progress[t.id];return p?.errors>0&&(!p.lastFirstTry||p.lastFirstTry<(p.lastErrorAt||0))&&now-(Number(p.lastErrorAt)||0)>=WAIT});
 if(pending.length)return pending.sort((a,b)=>(data.progress[a.id].lastAt||0)-(data.progress[b.id].lastAt||0))[0];
 const untouched=options.find(t=>!data.progress[t.id]);if(untouched)return untouched;
 return options.slice().sort((a,b)=>(Number(data.progress[a.id]?.lastAt)||0)-(Number(data.progress[b.id]?.lastAt)||0))[0];
}
function subject(){return $('v78Subject')?.value||'spanish'}

 $('v81Feedback').textContent=active.feedback||'';
 $('v81NextHint').disabled=active.done||active.hints>=q.hints.length;

 $('v81Next').hidden=!active.done;
 $('v81End').textContent='Close exercise';
 $('v78Input').placeholder=active.done?'You can ask the coach another question…':'Type your answer…';
}
function start(id,keepFocus=false){const task=getItem(id)||recommend(subject());if(!task)return;const summary=$('v81Summary');if(summary)summary.hidden=true;
 const sub=$('v78Subject');if(sub&&sub.value!==task.subject){sub.value=task.subject;sub.dispatchEvent(new Event('change',{bubbles:true}))}
 if(window.studyTableV78?.status?.().active||$('v78Topic')?.value!==task.topic)window.studyTableV78.chooseTopic(task.topic);
 active={task,wrong:0,hints:0,feedback:'',done:false,started:Date.now()};syncCard();
 if(!keepFocus)$('v78Input')?.focus();
}
function startNext(){if(!active){start();return}const arr=ITEMS.filter(t=>t.subject===active.task.subject);const idx=arr.findIndex(t=>t.id===active.task.id);const remaining=arr.filter(t=>t.id!==active.task.id&&!data.progress[t.id]);start(remaining[0]?.id||arr[(idx+1)%arr.length]?.id);}
function expose(){if(active){data.exposed[active.task.id]=Date.now();save()}}
function hint(){if(!active||active.done)return;active.hints=Math.min(active.hints+1,active.task.hints.length);expose();active.feedback='Use the clue, then write your own answer.';syncCard()}
function record(status,answerText){if(!active||active.done)return;const a=active,task=a.task,p=progress(task),now=Date.now();
 const firstTry=status==='correct'&&a.wrong===0&&a.hints===0&&now-(Number(data.exposed[task.id])||0)>=WAIT;
 p.attempts++;p.lastAt=now;p.lastStatus=status;
 if(firstTry){p.firstTry++;p.lastFirstTry=now}else p.assisted++;
 data.events.push({id:task.id,at:now,result:firstTry?'first-try':status,answer:String(answerText||'').slice(0,120)});data.events=data.events.slice(-90);
 data.exposed[task.id]=now;save();a.done=true;
 a.feedback=`${status==='revealed'?'Here’s the worked answer.':firstTry?'That’s right — on your own.':'That’s right. Let’s check it again another day.'}\n\n${task.worked}\n\n${task.note}`;syncCard()}
function submit(value){if(!active||active.done)return false;const a=active,task=a.task,result=evaluate(task,value);
 if(result.status==='empty')return true;
 if(result.status==='correct'){record('correct',value);return true}
 if(result.status==='wrong'){a.wrong++;progress(task).errors++;progress(task).lastErrorAt=Date.now();progress(task).lastAt=Date.now();expose();save();a.feedback=`Not quite. ${result.detail}\n\nTry again, or take a hint.`;syncCard();return true}
 if(result.status==='near'){a.wrong++;expose();a.feedback=result.detail;syncCard();return true}
 // Crucial: do not claim broad free-form responses are wrong when the local checker has not recognized them.
 a.feedback=`I can't check that wording confidently. It may be valid. You can try a shorter answer, or compare yours with the worked answer.\n\n${task.hints[0]}`;syncCard();return true;
}
function show(){if(!active||active.done)return;expose();record('revealed','')}
function stop(){active=null;syncCard();}
function review(){const c=counts();const due=ITEMS.filter(t=>{const p=data.progress[t.id];return p?.errors>0&&(!p.lastFirstTry||p.lastFirstTry<(p.lastErrorAt||0))&&Date.now()-(Number(p.lastErrorAt)||0)>=WAIT});const out=due.length?due.map(t=>`• ${t.subject}: ${t.question}`).slice(0,5).join('\n'):'Nothing due from these short-answer exercises yet.';
 active=null;syncCard();const panel=$('v81Summary');panel.hidden=false;panel.textContent=`Written practice\n${c.attempts} completed · ${c.firstTry} right on the first try · ${c.assisted} done with help or after seeing the answer\n\n${out}`;
}
function mount(){const room=$('v78Room'),bottom=$('v78Bottom'),form=$('v78Form');if(!room||!bottom||!form)return;

 const summary=document.createElement('button');summary.type='button';summary.textContent='My written practice';summary.id='v81SummaryBtn';summary.onclick=review;bottom.querySelector('.v78-chips')?.append(summary);
 const card=document.createElement('section');card.id='v81Card';card.hidden=true;card.setAttribute('aria-label','Written practice');card.innerHTML='<div id="v81Head"><strong id="v81Title"></strong><button type="button" id="v81End">Close exercise</button></div><p id="v81Question"></p><p id="v81Hint"></p><p id="v81Feedback" role="status" aria-live="polite"></p><div id="v81Controls"><button type="button" id="v81NextHint">Give me a hint</button><button type="button" id="v81Show">Show the answer</button><button type="button" id="v81Next" hidden>Next question →</button></div>';
 bottom.insertBefore(card,bottom.firstChild);
 const box=document.createElement('div');box.id='v81Summary';box.hidden=true;box.setAttribute('role','status');bottom.insertBefore(box,card.nextSibling);
 $('v81NextHint').onclick=hint;$('v81Show').onclick=show;$('v81Next').onclick=startNext;$('v81End').onclick=stop;
 const prev=form.onsubmit;form.onsubmit=e=>{if(active&&!active.done){e.preventDefault();const box=$('v78Input'),val=box.value.trim();if(val){box.value='';submit(val)}return}if(active?.done){stop()}return prev?.call(form,e)};
 $('v78Subject').addEventListener('change',()=>{stop();$('v81Summary').hidden=true});
 $('v78Topic').addEventListener('change',()=>{stop();$('v81Summary').hidden=true});
 room.addEventListener('click',e=>{if(!active)return;const btn=e.target.closest('button');if(btn&&btn.closest('.v78-chips')&&btn.id!=='v81Start'&&btn.id!=='v81SummaryBtn'){stop()}},true);
 const origOpen=window.studyTableV78.open;
 window.studyTableV78.open=function(...args){const result=origOpen(...args);if(active)syncCard();return result};
 window.studyTableV81={version:81,items:ITEMS.map(x=>({id:x.id,subject:x.subject,question:x.question})),open:(id)=>{window.studyTableV78.open({subject:getItem(id)?.subject||subject()});start(id)},start,submit,hint,show,stop,review,read:load,evaluate:(id,v)=>{const item=getItem(id);return item?evaluate(item,v):null},status:()=>({active:active?.task.id||null,done:!!active?.done,feedback:active?.feedback||'',hints:active?.hints||0,counts:counts()})};
}
if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',mount,{once:true});else mount();
})();

// V84: optional reasoning notebook. Writing does not change independent scores.
const V84_KEY='studytable_v84_case_notebook';
function v84Read(){try{const o=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(V84_KEY)||'{}');return o&&typeof o==='object'&&!Array.isArray(o)?o:{}}catch{return {}}}
let v84Journal=v84Read();v84Journal.drafts=v84Journal.drafts||{};v84Journal.history=v84Journal.history||{};
function v84Save(){try{localStorage.setItem(V84_KEY,JSON.stringify(v84Journal))}catch{}}
function v84DraftKey(){return current.id+':'+stepIndex}
function v84Mount(){if(!current)return;const host=$('v83Body').querySelector('.v83-work');if(!host)return;
 const block=document.createElement('section');block.className='v84-notebook';block.innerHTML='<h4>What do you think?</h4><p>Put your reasoning into words before you choose. This is optional, and you can change your mind.</p><textarea id="v84Reason" maxlength="1600" aria-label="Your reasoning" placeholder="I think ... because the evidence shows ..."></textarea><small>Only you can see these notes on this device.</small>';
 const actions=host.querySelector('.v83-actions');host.insertBefore(block,actions);
 const input=$('v84Reason');input.value=String(v84Journal.drafts[v84DraftKey()]||'').slice(0,1600);
 input.addEventListener('input',()=>{v84Journal.drafts[v84DraftKey()]=input.value;v84Save()});
}
function v84Compare(){if(!current)return;const f=$('v83Feedback');if(!f)return;const q=current.steps[stepIndex],written=String(v84Journal.drafts[v84DraftKey()]||'').trim();
 const block=document.createElement('div');block.className='v84-compare';
 block.innerHTML='<strong>Compare your reasoning</strong>'+(written?'<p><b>You wrote:</b> '+esc(written)+'</p>':'<p>You can still write down how you would explain this.')+'<p><b>The key idea:</b> '+esc(q.why)+'</p>';
 const old=v84Journal.history[current.id];if(old&&old[stepIndex]){const d=document.createElement('details');d.className='v84-previous';d.innerHTML='<summary>Read your notes from last time</summary><p>'+esc(old[stepIndex])+'</p>';block.appendChild(d)}
 f.appendChild(block);
 const input=$('v84Reason');if(input){input.disabled=true;input.setAttribute('aria-label','Your reasoning for this completed question')}
}
function v84Finish(){if(!current)return;const notes=[0,1,2].map(i=>String(v84Journal.drafts[current.id+':'+i]||'').trim());
 if(notes.some(Boolean)){v84Journal.history[current.id]=notes;const section=document.createElement('section');section.className='v84-summary';section.innerHTML='<h4>Your case notes</h4>'+notes.map((n,i)=>n?'<p><b>Clue '+(i+1)+':</b> '+esc(n)+'</p>':'').join('');const target=$('v83Body').querySelector('.v83-finish');target?.insertAdjacentElement('afterend',section)}
 for(let i=0;i<3;i++)delete v84Journal.drafts[current.id+':'+i];v84Save();
}

const KEY='studytable_v83_cases',DAY=86400000,WAIT=12*3600000;
const esc=s=>String(s??'').replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
const read=()=>{try{const o=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(KEY)||'{}');return o&&typeof o==='object'&&!Array.isArray(o)?o:{}}catch{return {}}};
const initial=()=>{const r=read();return {version:1,records:r.records&&typeof r.records==='object'&&!Array.isArray(r.records)?r.records:{},recent:Array.isArray(r.recent)?r.recent.slice(-100):[],subject:r.subject==='history'?'history':'medicine'}};
// V85: remember the reasoning behind missed choices, without grading free-form notes.
const V85_KEY='studytable_v85_case_review';
function v85Read(){try{const v=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(V85_KEY)||'{}');return v&&typeof v==='object'&&!Array.isArray(v)?v:{}}catch{return {}}}
let v85Log=v85Read(),v85Current=[];
function v85Save(){try{localStorage.setItem(V85_KEY,JSON.stringify(v85Log))}catch{}}
function v85Record(type,option){if(!current)return;const q=current.steps[stepIndex];let r=v85Current.find(x=>x.step===stepIndex);if(!r){r={step:stepIndex,question:q.q,issues:[]};v85Current.push(r)}
 if(type==='wrong'&&Number.isInteger(option)&&option!==q.correct&&!r.issues.some(x=>x.type==='wrong'&&x.option===option))r.issues.push({type:'wrong',option,choice:q.opts[option],reason:q.feedback[option]||q.why});
 if(type==='hint'&&!r.issues.some(x=>x.type==='hint'))r.issues.push({type:'hint',reason:q.hint});
 if(type==='reveal'&&!r.issues.some(x=>x.type==='reveal'))r.issues.push({type:'reveal',reason:q.why});
}
function v85Debrief(c){const entries=v85Current.filter(x=>x.issues.length);const target=$('v83Body').querySelector('.v83-finish');if(!target)return;
 const box=document.createElement('section');box.className='v85-debrief';
 let html='<h4>What to remember</h4>';
 if(!entries.length){html+='<p>You worked through every decision without a hint or correction. Try another case, or return to this one after a break.</p>'}
 else {html+='<p>These are the ideas worth revisiting before your next attempt.</p>';
 html+=entries.map(x=>'<div class="v85-point"><strong>Question '+(x.step+1)+': '+esc(x.question)+'</strong>'+x.issues.map(i=>i.type==='wrong'?'<p><b>Your choice:</b> '+esc(i.choice)+'</p><p><b>What it missed:</b> '+esc(i.reason)+'</p>':i.type==='hint'?'<p><b>The clue you used:</b> '+esc(i.reason)+'</p>':'<p><b>You looked at the answer:</b> '+esc(i.reason)+'</p>').join('')+'<p><b>Remember:</b> '+esc(c.steps[x.step].why)+'</p></div>').join('')}
 box.innerHTML=html;target.insertAdjacentElement('afterend',box);
 v85Log[c.id]={at:Date.now(),issues:entries.map(x=>({step:x.step,question:x.question,issues:x.issues.map(i=>({...i})),takeaway:c.steps[x.step].why})),title:c.title};v85Save();
}
function v85Past(c){const old=v85Log[c.id];if(!old||!Array.isArray(old.issues)||!old.issues.length)return '';return '<details class="v85-past"><summary>Read what tripped you up last time</summary>'+old.issues.map(x=>'<p><b>Question '+(x.step+1)+':</b> '+esc(x.takeaway||'')+'</p>').join('')+'</details>'}
let data=initial(),mode='menu',subject=data.subject,selected=null,current=null,stepIndex=0,helped=false,wrong=false,answerShown=false,caseSupported=false,lastFocus=null,oldOverflow='';
const tally=(c)=>{const r=data.records[c.id]||{};return {runs:Number(r.runs)||0,clean:Number(r.clean)||0,supported:Number(r.supported)||0,later:Number(r.later)||0,last: Number(r.last)||0,due:Number(r.due)||0,exposure:Number(r.exposure)||0,mode:r.mode||''}};
function save(){try{localStorage.setItem(KEY,JSON.stringify(data));return true}catch{return false}}
function ready(c){const s=tally(c),now=Date.now();if(s.runs&&s.due<=now)return {priority:4,text:'Time to revisit'};if(!s.runs)return {priority:3,text:'New case'};if(s.mode==='supported')return {priority:2,text:'Try it again without help'};return {priority:1,text:'Completed · revisit later'}}
function recommended(){return CASES.filter(c=>c.subject===subject).slice().sort((a,b)=>{const x=ready(a),y=ready(b);return y.priority-x.priority||tally(a).last-tally(b).last})[0]}
const heading=()=>subject==='history'?'History cases':'Medicine cases';
const overlay=document.createElement('div');overlay.id='v83Overlay';overlay.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');overlay.innerHTML='<section id="v83Room" tabindex="-1" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby="v83Heading"><header id="v83Top"><div><div class="v83-brand">STUDY TABLE / THE CASE ROOM</div><h2 id="v83Heading">Think it through</h2></div><button type="button" id="v83Close" aria-label="Close case room">Close ×</button></header><main id="v83Body"></main></section>';
function scrollUp(){$('v83Room').scrollTo({top:0,behavior:'instant'})}
function open(s,id){if(s==='history'||s==='medicine')subject=s;data.subject=subject;save();lastFocus=document.activeElement;oldOverflow=document.body.style.overflow;document.body.appendChild(overlay);overlay.dataset.open='1';overlay.setAttribute('aria-hidden','false');document.body.style.overflow='hidden';if(id){const c=CASES.find(x=>x.id===id);if(c){subject=c.subject;start(c);return}}mode='menu';menu();$('v83Room').focus()}
function close(){overlay.dataset.open='0';overlay.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');document.body.style.overflow=oldOverflow;lastFocus?.focus?.()}
function switchSubject(s){if(subject===s)return;subject=s;data.subject=s;save();mode='menu';menu()}
function tabs(){return `<div class="v83-switch" role="group" aria-label="Choose case subject"><button type="button" data-v83sub="medicine" aria-pressed="${subject==='medicine'}">Medicine</button><button type="button" data-v83sub="history" aria-pressed="${subject==='history'}">History</button></div>`}
function menu(){mode='menu';const top=recommended(),s=tally(top),r=ready(top);const list=CASES.filter(c=>c.subject===subject);$('v83Body').innerHTML=`<span class="v83-kicker">CHOOSE A CASE</span><h3>Find out what the evidence tells you.</h3><p class="v83-muted">Follow three clues. Decide what they mean. Get an explanation whenever you need one.</p>${tabs()}<section class="v83-feature"><span class="v83-kicker">${esc(r.text)}</span><h3>${esc(top.title)}</h3><p>${esc(top.dek)}</p><button type="button" class="v83-button" id="v83Recommended">${s.runs?'Try this case again':'Start this case'} →</button></section><p class="v83-kicker">OTHER CASES</p><div class="v83-cards">${list.filter(x=>x!==top).map(c=>{const r=ready(c),p=tally(c);return `<button type="button" class="v83-card" data-v83case="${esc(c.id)}"><small>${esc(r.text)}${p.later?' · Answered again on a later day':''}</small><strong>${esc(c.title)}</strong><span>${esc(c.dek)}</span></button>`}).join('')}</div><p class="v83-muted" style="margin-top:18px">${subject==='medicine'?'These simplified cases teach reasoning, not diagnosis or treatment of a real patient.':'Source cards are teaching descriptions, not archival quotations. They show how to question evidence.'}</p>`;
 $('v83Recommended').onclick=()=>start(top);$('v83Body').querySelectorAll('[data-v83case]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>start(CASES.find(c=>c.id===b.dataset.v83case)));bindTabs();scrollUp()}
function bindTabs(){$('v83Body').querySelectorAll('[data-v83sub]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>switchSubject(b.dataset.v83sub))}
function start(c){if(!c)return;current=c;subject=c.subject;data.subject=subject;save();mode='case';v85Current=[];selected=null;stepIndex=0;helped=false;wrong=false;answerShown=false;caseSupported=false;renderStep();scrollUp()}
function renderStep(){if(!current)return;mode='case';const c=current,q=c.steps[stepIndex],p=Math.round(100*stepIndex/3);const order=[0,1,2];for(let i=order.length-1;i>0;i--){const j=Math.floor(Math.random()*(i+1));[order[i],order[j]]=[order[j],order[i]]} $('v83Body').innerHTML=`<button type="button" class="v83-plain" id="v83Back">← All ${esc(heading().toLowerCase())}</button><div class="v83-progress" aria-label="${stepIndex} of 3 steps completed"><div style="width:${p}%"></div></div><span class="v83-kicker">${esc(subject.toUpperCase())} · QUESTION ${stepIndex+1} OF 3</span><h3>${esc(c.title)}</h3><p class="v83-muted">${esc(c.dek)}</p><div class="v83-two-col"><aside class="v83-evidence"><h4>The situation</h4><p>${esc(c.setup)}</p>${c.steps.slice(0,stepIndex+1).map((s,i)=>`<div class="v83-clue"><strong>${i===stepIndex?'NEW CLUE':'EARLIER CLUE'}</strong>${esc(s.evidence)}</div>`).join('')}</aside><section class="v83-work"><span class="v83-kicker">YOUR DECISION</span><p class="v83-q">${esc(q.q)}</p><div class="v83-choices" role="group" aria-label="Possible answers">${order.map(i=>`<button type="button" data-v83choice="${i}" aria-pressed="false">${esc(q.opts[i])}</button>`).join('')}</div>${stepIndex===0?v85Past(c):''}<div class="v83-actions"><button type="button" class="v83-button" id="v83Check" disabled>Check my answer</button><button type="button" class="v83-light" id="v83Hint">Give me a clue</button><button type="button" class="v83-plain" id="v83Reveal">Show me why</button></div><div id="v83Feedback" aria-live="polite"></div></section></div>`;
 $('v83Back').onclick=menu;$('v83Body').querySelectorAll('[data-v83choice]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>select(+b.dataset.v83choice));$('v83Check').onclick=check;$('v83Hint').onclick=hint;$('v83Reveal').onclick=reveal;v84Mount();}
function select(i){if(answerShown||!current)return;selected=i;$('v83Body').querySelectorAll('[data-v83choice]').forEach(b=>b.setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(+b.dataset.v83choice===i)));$('v83Check').disabled=false}
function hint(){if(!current||answerShown)return;v85Record('hint');helped=true;caseSupported=true;const q=current.steps[stepIndex];$('v83Feedback').className='v83-feedback';$('v83Feedback').innerHTML=`<strong>A clue</strong><p>${esc(q.hint)}</p>`;$('v83Feedback').scrollIntoView({block:'nearest'})}
function check(){if(!current||answerShown||selected===null)return;const q=current.steps[stepIndex];if(selected!==q.correct){v85Record('wrong',selected);wrong=true;caseSupported=true;$('v83Feedback').className='v83-feedback v83-wrong';$('v83Feedback').innerHTML=`<strong>Not quite.</strong><p>${esc(q.feedback[selected])}</p><p>Try another answer, or read the explanation.</p>`;$('v83Body').querySelectorAll('[data-v83choice]').forEach(b=>b.setAttribute('aria-pressed','false'));selected=null;$('v83Check').disabled=true;$('v83Feedback').scrollIntoView({block:'nearest'});return}settle(false)}
function reveal(){if(!current||answerShown)return;v85Record('reveal');caseSupported=true;settle(true)}
function settle(revealed){const q=current.steps[stepIndex];answerShown=true;const correct=q.correct;
 $('v83Body').querySelectorAll('[data-v83choice]').forEach(b=>{b.disabled=true;b.setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(+b.dataset.v83choice===correct));if(+b.dataset.v83choice===correct)b.classList.add('v83-right')});$('v83Check').disabled=true;$('v83Hint').disabled=true;$('v83Reveal').disabled=true;
 const clean=!revealed&&!helped&&!wrong;const tag=clean?'On your own':revealed?'Worked answer':'With some help';const f=$('v83Feedback');f.className='v83-feedback';f.innerHTML=`<strong>${tag}</strong><p>${esc(q.opts[correct])}</p><p>${esc(q.why)}</p><div class="v83-actions"><button type="button" class="v83-button" id="v83Next">${stepIndex===2?'Finish case':'Next clue'} →</button></div>`;
 v84Compare();f.scrollIntoView({block:'nearest'});$('v83Next').onclick=()=>{if(stepIndex===2)finish();else{stepIndex++;selected=null;helped=false;wrong=false;answerShown=false;renderStep();$('v83Room').scrollTo({top:0,behavior:'instant'})}};
}
function finish(){if(!current)return;mode='done';const c=current,now=Date.now(),independent=!caseSupported,row=data.records[c.id]||{runs:0,clean:0,supported:0,later:0};const hadExposure=Number(row.exposure)||0;
 const later=independent&&hadExposure>0&&now-hadExposure>=WAIT;
 row.runs=(Number(row.runs)||0)+1;row.clean=(Number(row.clean)||0)+(independent?1:0);row.supported=(Number(row.supported)||0)+(independent?0:1);row.later=(Number(row.later)||0)+(later?1:0);row.last=now;row.mode=independent?'clean':'supported';row.due=now+(independent?3*DAY:WAIT);row.exposure=now;data.records[c.id]=row;
 data.recent.push({id:c.id,at:now,independent,later,assisted:!independent});data.recent=data.recent.slice(-100);const saved=save();
 $('v83Body').innerHTML=`<button type="button" class="v83-plain" id="v83Back">← All ${esc(heading().toLowerCase())}</button><span class="v83-kicker">CASE COMPLETE</span><h3>${esc(c.title)}</h3><div class="v83-finish"><strong>${later?'You remembered this after a break.':independent?'You worked through all three clues without help.':'You worked through the case.'}</strong><p>${esc(c.ending)}</p><p>${esc(c.bridge)}</p></div><div class="v83-stat">${independent?'Three first-try answers with no hints.':'This attempt included a hint, correction, or revealed explanation.'}${later?' You also answered after at least 12 hours away from the worked answer.':''} ${saved?'Your attempt was saved.':'This browser could not save your attempt.'}</div><div class="v83-actions"><button type="button" class="v83-button" id="v83Another">Try another case →</button><button type="button" class="v83-light" id="v83Replay">Replay this case</button><button type="button" class="v83-light" id="v83Course">Go to the full course</button></div>`;
 v84Finish();v85Debrief(c);$('v83Back').onclick=menu;$('v83Another').onclick=()=>{mode='menu';menu()};$('v83Replay').onclick=()=>start(c);$('v83Course').onclick=()=>{const target=c.subject;close();if(window.studyTableV62?.open)window.studyTableV62.open(target);else window.v35Open?.(target)};scrollUp()}
function addLaunch(){const home=$('v62HomeFeature')||document.querySelector('#v35Portal .v36-home-hero');if(!home)return;const target=home.querySelector('.v65-more-actions')||home.querySelector('.v65-more')||home; if(!target.querySelector('#v83HomeLaunch')){const b=document.createElement('button');b.type='button';b.className='v83-launch';b.id='v83HomeLaunch';b.textContent='Explore a case: medicine or history';b.onclick=()=>open();target.appendChild(b)} }
let scheduled=false;
function sync(){if(scheduled)return;scheduled=true;requestAnimationFrame(()=>{scheduled=false;addLaunch()})}
const observer=new MutationObserver(sync);
function init(){if(!$('v83Overlay'))document.body.appendChild(overlay);$('v83Close').onclick=close;overlay.addEventListener('click',e=>{if(e.target===overlay)close()});document.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(overlay.dataset.open==='1'&&e.key==='Escape'){e.preventDefault();close()}if(overlay.dataset.open==='1'&&e.key==='Tab'){const buttons=[...overlay.querySelectorAll('button:not([disabled])')].filter(b=>b.getClientRects().length);if(buttons.length&&e.shiftKey&&document.activeElement===buttons[0]){e.preventDefault();buttons[buttons.length-1].focus()}else if(buttons.length&&!e.shiftKey&&document.activeElement===buttons[buttons.length-1]){e.preventDefault();buttons[0].focus()}}});observer.observe(document.querySelector('#v35Portal')||document.body,{subtree:true,childList:true});sync()}
window.studyTableV83={version:83,open,close,start:(id)=>open(undefined,id),data:CASES.map(({id,subject,title,steps})=>({id,subject,title,count:steps.length})),read:initial,status:id=>tally(CASES.find(c=>c.id===id)||CASES[0]),recommend:(s)=>{if(s==='history'||s==='medicine'){subject=s}return recommended()?.id}};
if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',init,{once:true});else init();
})();

</script>

 ['You eat breakfast.','comer','tú','present','comes','Present: -er verbs take -es with tú.','Por las mañanas, tú ___ algo.'],

 ['They eat at home.','comer','ellos','present','comen','Present: -er verbs take -en with ellos.','Ellos ___ en casa.'],
 ['I go to the park.','ir','yo','present','voy','Ir is irregular: yo voy.','Yo ___ al parque.'],
 ['You have time.','tener','tú','present','tienes','Tener changes e to ie with tú.','¿Tú ___ tiempo?'],
 ['She is tired.','estar','ella','present','está','Estar: ella está. The accent changes the written form.','Ella ___ cansada.'],
 ['I spoke yesterday.','hablar','yo','preterite','hablé','Preterite: -ar verbs take -é with yo.','Ayer yo ___ con Ana.'],

 ['We talked last night.','hablar','nosotros','preterite','hablamos','Hablamos is present or past. Anoche tells you it is past.','Anoche nosotros ___.'],
 ['They ate dinner.','comer','ellos','preterite','comieron','Preterite: -er verbs take -ieron with ellos.','Anoche ellos ___ juntos.'],
 ['I went yesterday.','ir','yo','preterite','fui','Ir has an irregular preterite: yo fui.','Ayer yo ___ al museo.'],

 ['I used to speak Spanish.','hablar','yo','imperfect','hablaba','Imperfect: -ar verbs take -aba with yo.','Cuando era joven, yo ___ español.'],

 ['She used to live here.','vivir','ella','imperfect','vivía','Imperfect: -ir verbs take -ía with ella.','Antes, ella ___ aquí.'],
 ['I was a child.','ser','yo','imperfect','era','Ser is irregular in the imperfect: yo era.','Yo ___ pequeño entonces.'],
 ['They were going to school.','ir','ellos','imperfect','iban','Ir is irregular in the imperfect: ellos iban.','Cada mañana, ellos ___ a la escuela.'],
 ['I will speak tomorrow.','hablar','yo','future','hablaré','Future: keep the whole infinitive and add -é.','Mañana yo ___ con ellos.'],
 ['She will eat later.','comer','ella','future','comerá','Future: keep the whole infinitive and add -á.','Después, ella ___.'],
 ['We will live here.','vivir','nosotros','future','viviremos','Future: keep the whole infinitive and add -emos.','El año que viene nosotros ___ aquí.'],
