];
const extraSpanish=[
 ['I want a coffee.','querer','yo','present','quiero','Querer changes e to ie: quiero.','En la cafetería: «___ un café, por favor».'],
 ['Can you help me?','poder','tú','present','puedes','Poder changes o to ue with tú: puedes.','«¿Me ___ ayudar?»'],
 ['I know the answer.','saber','yo','present','sé','Saber is irregular for yo: sé.','«Yo ___ la respuesta».'],
 ['We go to work on Mondays.','ir','nosotros','present','vamos','Ir is irregular: nosotros vamos.','Los lunes nosotros ___ al trabajo.'],
 ['He does his homework.','hacer','él','present','hace','Hacer: él hace; the yo form is hago.','Él ___ la tarea por la tarde.'],
 ['I say hello.','decir','yo','present','digo','Decir is irregular for yo: digo.','Cuando entro, yo ___ hola.'],
 ['Yesterday I bought bread.','comprar','yo','preterite','compré','A completed event yesterday takes the preterite; yo -é.','Ayer yo ___ pan.'],
 ['She arrived at eight.','llegar','ella','preterite','llegó','A single completed arrival takes the preterite; ella -ó.','Ayer ella ___ a las ocho.'],
 ['We saw the film last night.','ver','nosotros','preterite','vimos','Ver has short preterite forms: nosotros vimos.','Anoche nosotros ___ la película.'],
 ['I made dinner.','hacer','yo','preterite','hice','Hacer has the irregular preterite: yo hice.','Ayer yo ___ la cena.'],
 ['They said yes.','decir','ellos','preterite','dijeron','Decir has the irregular preterite: ellos dijeron.','Al final ellos ___ que sí.'],
 ['You were at the party last night.','estar','tú','preterite','estuviste','Estar has the irregular stem estuv- in the preterite.','Anoche tú ___ en la fiesta.'],
 ['When I was young, I played outside.','jugar','yo','imperfect','jugaba','The imperfect describes a repeated childhood habit.','De niño, yo ___ afuera.'],
 ['She always read before bed.','leer','ella','imperfect','leía','Imperfect -ía marks a past habit; preserve the accent.','Antes de dormir, ella siempre ___.'],
 ['We had little money back then.','tener','nosotros','imperfect','teníamos','Tener is regular in the imperfect; nosotros -íamos.','En esa época nosotros ___ poco dinero.'],
 ['It was raining while I studied.','llover','él','imperfect','llovía','Imperfect describes weather in progress: llovía.','Mientras estudiaba, ___.'],
 ['I was tired every Friday.','estar','yo','imperfect','estaba','Imperfect describes a recurring state: yo estaba.','Todos los viernes yo ___ cansado.'],
 ['You were happy as a child.','ser','tú','imperfect','eras','Ser is irregular in the imperfect: tú eras.','De pequeño, tú ___ feliz.'],
 ['We will arrive tomorrow.','llegar','nosotros','future','llegaremos','Add -emos to the whole infinitive: llegaremos.','Mañana nosotros ___ temprano.'],
 ['They will come next week.','venir','ellos','future','vendrán','Venir has the irregular future stem vendr-.','La semana que viene ellos ___.'],
 ['I will do it tomorrow.','hacer','yo','future','haré','Hacer shortens to har- in the future: haré.','Mañana yo lo ___.'],
 ['She will be able to join us.','poder','ella','future','podrá','Poder uses the future stem podr-: podrá.','Mañana ella ___ venir.'],
 ['You will tell me later.','decir','tú','future','dirás','Decir shortens to dir- in the future: dirás.','Después tú me lo ___.'],
 ['I will leave at nine.','salir','yo','future','saldré','Salir uses the future stem saldr-.','Mañana yo ___ a las nueve.']
];
const QUESTIONS=oldSpanish.concat(extraSpanish).map((a,i)=>({id:`${a[1]}-${a[2]}-${a[3]}`,en:a[0],verb:a[1],person:a[2],tense:a[3],answer:a[4],why:a[5],sentence:a[6],old:i<24}));
// All questions should have stable unique IDs, including the original 24 prompts.
if(new Set(QUESTIONS.map(q=>q.id)).size!==QUESTIONS.length)throw Error('Spanish exercise IDs must be distinct');

// Paired stories teach the difference between a completed event and a repeated/background past.
const PAST_STORIES=[["Ayer ___ con Ana por teléfono.","Yesterday I spoke with Ana on the phone.","hablar","yo","hablé","hablaba","preterite","Ayer frames a completed conversation. Use hablé, not the habitual/background hablaba."],["Cuando era niño, ___ con mi abuelo todos los domingos.","When I was a child, I used to speak with my grandfather every Sunday.","hablar","yo","hablaba","hablé","imperfect","Todos los domingos describes a repeated past habit. Hablaba fits; hablé sounds like a finished occasion."],["Anoche ___ tarde porque llegué tarde a casa.","Last night I ate late because I got home late.","comer","yo","comí","comía","preterite","Anoche points to that completed meal. Comí is the preterite."],["Antes ___ a las ocho todas las noches.","I used to eat at eight every night.","comer","yo","comía","comí","imperfect","Antes and todas las noches describe the routine. Comía is the imperfect."],["El año pasado ella ___ en Lima durante seis meses y luego volvió.","Last year she lived in Lima for six months and then returned.","vivir","ella","vivió","vivía","preterite","The six-month stay is presented as a complete period, followed by her return: vivió."],["Cuando era pequeña, ella ___ cerca de la playa.","When she was little, she lived near the beach.","vivir","ella","vivía","vivió","imperfect","This sets the scene of her childhood rather than narrating the end of the stay: vivía."],["Ayer nosotros ___ al museo por primera vez.","Yesterday we went to the museum for the first time.","ir","nosotros","fuimos","íbamos","preterite","Por primera vez describes a distinct visit that happened yesterday: fuimos."],["De pequeños, nosotros ___ al museo cada mes.","As children, we used to go to the museum every month.","ir","nosotros","íbamos","fuimos","imperfect","Cada mes makes this a repeated childhood routine: íbamos."],["El viernes ___ la cena para mis amigos.","On Friday I made dinner for my friends.","hacer","yo","hice","hacía","preterite","The dinner is one completed event on Friday: hice."],["Antes ___ la cena casi todas las noches.","I used to make dinner almost every night.","hacer","yo","hacía","hice","imperfect","Casi todas las noches signals a past habit: hacía."],["Ayer tú ___ tres reuniones seguidas.","Yesterday you had three meetings in a row.","tener","tú","tuviste","tenías","preterite","This refers to three actual meetings yesterday: tuviste."],["De niño, tú ___ un perro llamado Max.","When you were a child, you had a dog named Max.","tener","tú","tenías","tuviste","imperfect","The dog is background information about your childhood: tenías."],["Esta mañana ella ___ al trabajo a las siete.","This morning she arrived at work at seven.","llegar","ella","llegó","llegaba","preterite","A specific arrival at seven this morning is a completed event: llegó."],["Durante años ella ___ al trabajo a las siete.","For years she used to arrive at work at seven.","llegar","ella","llegaba","llegó","imperfect","Durante años frames a recurring pattern, not one arrival: llegaba."],["Anoche ellos ___ una película completa.","Last night they watched a whole film.","ver","ellos","vieron","veían","preterite","They watched a complete film on one occasion: vieron."],["Los domingos ellos ___ películas con su familia.","On Sundays they used to watch films with their family.","ver","ellos","veían","vieron","imperfect","Los domingos describes what they habitually did: veían."]].map((a,i)=>({id:'past-story-'+i,sentence:a[0],en:a[1],verb:a[2],person:a[3],answer:a[4],contrast:a[5],tense:a[6],why:a[7]}));
const WHY_TENSE={present:'This is happening now or regularly.',preterite:'The sentence presents a past action as a completed event.',imperfect:'The sentence describes a past routine, an ongoing situation, or background.',future:'The sentence points to something that has not happened yet.'};
function currentSpanish(){return spanishKind==='past'?PAST_STORIES[index]:QUESTIONS[index]}

const WHITE=[{name:'C4',note:'C',midi:60},{name:'D4',note:'D',midi:62},{name:'E4',note:'E',midi:64},{name:'F4',note:'F',midi:65},{name:'G4',note:'G',midi:67},{name:'A4',note:'A',midi:69},{name:'B4',note:'B',midi:71},{name:'C5',note:'C',midi:72}];
const BLACK=[{name:'C#4',midi:61,slot:0},{name:'D#4',midi:63,slot:1},{name:'F#4',midi:66,slot:3},{name:'G#4',midi:68,slot:4},{name:'A#4',midi:70,slot:5}];
const PITCHES=[...WHITE,...BLACK];
const TUNES=[
 {name:'Mary Had a Little Lamb',notes:['E4','D4','C4','D4','E4','E4','E4','D4','D4','D4','E4','G4','G4'],tip:'Start with E, then walk down to D and C.'},
 {name:'Ode to Joy',notes:['E4','E4','F4','G4','G4','F4','E4','D4','C4','C4','D4','E4','E4','D4','D4'],tip:'Most of this tune moves one white key at a time.'},
 {name:'Twinkle, Twinkle',notes:['C4','C4','G4','G4','A4','A4','G4','F4','F4','E4','E4','D4','D4','C4'],tip:'The first jump is from C to G. Then step upward to A.'}
];
const EAR=[['C4','E4'],['G4','D4'],['E4','E4'],['D4','G4'],['A4','F4'],['C4','C5'],['F4','A4'],['G4','G4'],['B4','E4'],['C5','B4'],['D4','E4'],['A4','G4'],['E4','F4'],['F4','F4'],['G4','C5'],['B4','G4'],['F4','E4'],['E4','C4']].map(([a,b],i)=>({id:`ear-${i}`,a,b,correct:(PITCHES.find(x=>x.name===b).midi>PITCHES.find(x=>x.name===a).midi?'higher':PITCHES.find(x=>x.name===b).midi<PITCHES.find(x=>x.name===a).midi?'lower':'same')}));
const $=id=>document.getElementById(id), esc=s=>String(s).replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
const clean=s=>String(s).trim().toLowerCase().normalize('NFC').replace(/\s+/g,' ');
const bare=s=>clean(s).normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g,'');
function load(){try{const v=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(STORE)||'{}');return v&&typeof v==='object'&&!Array.isArray(v)?v:{}}catch{return {}}}
let progress=load(),mode='spanish',spanishKind='verbs',index=0,position=0,tune=0,tries=0,helped=false,revealed=false,done=false,feedback='',audio=null,lastFocus=null,rounds=[],spanishQueue=[],earQueue=[],showGuide=true,memoryMode=false,guideSeen=true,audioTimers=[];
function save(){try{localStorage.setItem(STORE,JSON.stringify(progress))}catch{}}
function group(mode){return progress[mode]??={}}
function studyId(){if(mode==='spanish')return currentSpanish().id;if(mode==='ear')return EAR[index].id;return `tune-${tune}`}
function logAttempt(wasClean,failed=false){const bucket=group(mode==='spanish'&&spanishKind==='past'?'past':mode),id=studyId(),prev=bucket[id]||{};
 bucket[id]={...prev,attempted:true,clean:!!(prev.clean||wasClean),attempts:Math.min(10000,(prev.attempts||0)+1),misses:Math.min(10000,(prev.misses||0)+(failed?1:0)),assisted:!!(prev.assisted||helped||revealed||tries>0),last:Date.now()}; save();}
function total(){const b=group(mode==='spanish'&&spanishKind==='past'?'past':mode);const vals=Object.entries(b).filter(([id,v])=>v&&typeof v==='object'&&(mode!=='piano'||id.startsWith('tune-'))).map(([,v])=>v);return `${vals.filter(x=>x.clean).length} without help · ${vals.filter(x=>x.attempted).length} practiced`}
function shuffle(a){const b=a.slice();for(let i=b.length-1;i>0;i--){const j=Math.floor(Math.random()*(i+1));[b[i],b[j]]=[b[j],b[i]]}return b}
function getQueued(kind){const list=kind==='past'?PAST_STORIES.map((_,i)=>i):kind==='spanish'?QUESTIONS.map((_,i)=>i):EAR.map((_,i)=>i);
 const bucket=group(kind);return shuffle(list).sort((a,b)=>{const dataset=kind==='past'?PAST_STORIES:kind==='spanish'?QUESTIONS:EAR,x=dataset[a],y=dataset[b];const px=bucket[x.id],py=bucket[y.id];const xx=px&&!px.clean?0:px?1:0,yy=py&&!py.clean?0:py?1:0;return xx-yy})}
function reset(){cancelSequence();tries=0;helped=false;revealed=false;done=false;feedback='';position=0;showGuide=true;memoryMode=false;guideSeen=true}
function next(){reset();if(mode==='spanish'){if(!spanishQueue.length)spanishQueue=getQueued(spanishKind==='past'?'past':'spanish');index=spanishQueue.shift()}else if(mode==='ear'){if(!earQueue.length)earQueue=getQueued('ear');index=earQueue.shift()}else{tune=(tune+1)%TUNES.length}render()}
function open(which){if(!document.getElementById('v87Overlay'))return;progress=load();lastFocus=document.activeElement;mode=['spanish','piano','ear'].includes(which)?which:'spanish';reset();if(mode==='spanish'){spanishQueue=getQueued(spanishKind==='past'?'past':'spanish');index=spanishQueue.shift()}else if(mode==='ear'){earQueue=getQueued('ear');index=earQueue.shift()}else tune=0
 $('v87Overlay').classList.add('open');render();$('v87Close').focus()}
function close(){stop();$('v87Overlay').classList.remove('open');if(lastFocus&&lastFocus.isConnected&&typeof lastFocus.focus==='function')lastFocus.focus()}
function cancelSequence(){audioTimers.forEach(clearTimeout);audioTimers=[]}
function stop(){cancelSequence();if(audio&&audio.state==='running')audio.suspend().catch(()=>{})}
function sound(name){const n=PITCHES.find(p=>p.name===name);if(!n)return;try{const A=window.AudioContext||window.webkitAudioContext;if(!A){feedback='Sound is not available in this browser.';paintFeedback();return}if(!audio)audio=new A();audio.resume().then(()=>{const t=audio.currentTime;const f=440*Math.pow(2,(n.midi-69)/12);[1,2,3].forEach((part,i)=>{const osc=audio.createOscillator(),g=audio.createGain();osc.type='sine';osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(f*part,t);g.gain.setValueAtTime(.0001,t);g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime((.11/(part*part))*(i===0?1.2:1),t+.012);g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.0001,t+(.7-i*.12));osc.connect(g).connect(audio.destination);osc.start(t);osc.stop(t+.8)})}).catch(()=>{})}catch{feedback='Check your browser sound settings.';paintFeedback()}}
function sequence(names){cancelSequence();names.forEach((n,i)=>audioTimers.push(setTimeout(()=>{if($('v87Overlay')?.classList.contains('open'))sound(n)},i*680)))}
function keys(){const whites=WHITE.map(x=>`<button class="v87-white" data-v87-key="${x.name}" aria-label="${x.name}" type="button">${x.note}<small>${x.name.endsWith('5')?'5':'4'}</small></button>`).join('');const blacks=BLACK.map(x=>`<button class="v87-black" style="--slot:${x.slot}" type="button" data-v87-key="${x.name}" aria-label="${x.name}"><span>${x.name.replace('4','')}</span></button>`).join('');return `<div class="v87-keys" role="group" aria-label="One octave on a piano keyboard">${whites}${blacks}</div><p class="v87-keytip">The two black keys sit between C–D–E. The three black keys sit between F–G–A–B. The white key immediately before a pair is C.</p>`}
function nav(){return `<div class="v87-actions"><button id="v87Hint" type="button">A clue</button><button id="v87Reveal" type="button">Show answer</button><button id="v87Next" type="button">Next →</button></div><p class="v87-progress">${total()}</p><p class="v87-rule">Getting help is fine. Only a correct first try without help counts as solved on your own.</p>`}
function render(){const room=$('v87Main');if(!room)return;['spanish','piano','ear'].forEach(x=>$('v87Tab-'+x).setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(mode===x)));
 let out='';if(mode==='spanish'){
 const q=currentSpanish(),isPast=spanishKind==='past';
 out=`<div class="v88-spanish-modes"><button type="button" data-v88-spanish="verbs" aria-pressed="${!isPast}">Everyday verbs · 48</button><button type="button" data-v88-spanish="past" aria-pressed="${isPast}">Past-tense stories · 16</button></div><div class="v87-overline">Spanish · ${isPast?'Choose a past tense':'Say it naturally'}</div><h2>${esc(q.en)}</h2><p class="v87-spanish-sentence" lang="es">${esc(q.sentence)}</p><p>Write the <strong>${esc(q.verb)}</strong> form for <strong>${esc(q.person)}</strong>. Read the whole sentence before choosing.</p><form id="v87Form"><label for="v87Input">Your answer</label><div class="v87-inputrow"><input id="v87Input" spellcheck="false" autocapitalize="off" autocomplete="off" inputmode="text" placeholder="Write the verb"/><button type="submit">Check</button></div></form><div class="v87-accents" aria-label="Add a Spanish accent">${['á','é','í','ó','ú','ñ','ü'].map(c=>`<button type="button" data-v87-accent="${c}">${c}</button>`).join('')}</div><p class="v88-meaning">${isPast?'A completed event, or something that used to happen?':'Look for words that tell you when and how often it happens.'}</p>`;
 }else if(mode==='piano'){
 const t=TUNES[tune],goal=t.notes[position];out=`<div class="v87-overline">Piano · melody ${tune+1} of ${TUNES.length}</div><h2>${esc(t.name)}</h2><p>${memoryMode?'Play from memory. The next key stays hidden. If you get stuck, switch back to guided practice.':'Follow the highlighted keys first. When you are ready, start again without the answers showing.'}</p><div class="v87-melody"><span>Note ${Math.min(position+1,t.notes.length)} of ${t.notes.length}</span><strong id="v88Goal">${showGuide?esc(goal):'?'}</strong><button type="button" id="v87HearGoal" ${memoryMode?'hidden':''}>Hear this note</button></div>${keys()}<div class="v87-melody-controls"><button id="v87Guide" type="button" ${!memoryMode?'hidden':''}>${memoryMode?'Go back to guided practice':'Show the next note'}</button><button id="v88Memory" type="button">${memoryMode?'Restart from memory':'Start from memory'}</button><button id="v87ListenTune" type="button">Listen to the tune</button></div>${memoryMode?'':'<p>'+esc(t.tip)+'</p>'}`;
 }else{
 out=`<div class="v87-overline">Piano · listen for changes</div><h2>Did the second note go up or down?</h2><p>Listen to the two notes. You don't need perfect pitch—just hear whether the second note is higher, lower, or the same.</p><button type="button" class="v87-listen" id="v87Listen">▶ Hear the two notes</button><div class="v87-ear-choices">${[['higher','Higher ↑'],['lower','Lower ↓'],['same','Same note']].map(([x,n])=>`<button type="button" data-v87-choice="${x}">${n}</button>`).join('')}</div><p class="v87-keytip">Replay as often as you like. Hearing the relationship matters more than memorizing individual pitches.</p>`;
 }
 room.innerHTML=out+`<div id="v87Feedback" aria-live="polite" role="status" class="v87-feedback"></div>${nav()}`;
 $('v87Hint').onclick=hint;$('v87Reveal').onclick=reveal;$('v87Next').onclick=next;
 if(mode==='spanish'){room.querySelectorAll('[data-v88-spanish]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{spanishKind=b.dataset.v88Spanish;reset();spanishQueue=getQueued(spanishKind==='past'?'past':'spanish');index=spanishQueue.shift();render()});$('v87Form').onsubmit=checkSpanish;room.querySelectorAll('[data-v87-accent]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{const inp=$('v87Input'),at=inp.selectionStart??inp.value.length,bt=inp.selectionEnd??at;inp.value=inp.value.slice(0,at)+b.dataset.v87Accent+inp.value.slice(bt);inp.focus();inp.setSelectionRange(at+1,at+1)});$('v87Input').focus()}
 if(mode==='piano'){$('v87HearGoal').onclick=()=>{helped=true;sound(TUNES[tune].notes[position])};$('v87ListenTune').onclick=()=>{if(memoryMode)helped=true;sequence(TUNES[tune].notes)};$('v87Guide').onclick=()=>{if(memoryMode){reset();render()}else{helped=true;showGuide=true;guideSeen=true;paintGuide();feedback='The next key is highlighted.';paintFeedback()}};$('v88Memory').onclick=()=>{reset();memoryMode=true;showGuide=false;guideSeen=false;render()};room.querySelectorAll('[data-v87-key]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>pressKey(b.dataset.v87Key));paintGuide()}
 if(mode==='ear'){$('v87Listen').onclick=()=>sequence([EAR[index].a,EAR[index].b]);room.querySelectorAll('[data-v87-choice]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>answerEar(b.dataset.v87Choice))}
 paintFeedback();}
function paintFeedback(){if($('v87Feedback'))$('v87Feedback').innerHTML=feedback;const x=document.querySelector('#v87Main .v87-progress');if(x)x.textContent=total()}
function correct(content,cleanCredit){done=true;logAttempt(cleanCredit);feedback=`<strong>${content}</strong>`;paintFeedback()}
function checkSpanish(e){e.preventDefault();if(done)return;const q=currentSpanish(),v=clean($('v87Input').value),isPast=spanishKind==='past';if(!v){feedback='Try writing the verb first.';paintFeedback();return}
 if(v===clean(q.answer)){const why=isPast?q.why:`${WHY_TENSE[q.tense]} ${q.why}`;correct(`Yes: <em>${esc(q.answer)}</em>. ${esc(why)}`,tries===0&&!helped&&!revealed);return}
 tries++;logAttempt(false,true);
 if(bare(v)===bare(q.answer)){feedback=`Nearly. The accent matters: write <strong>${esc(q.answer)}</strong>, not <del>${esc(v)}</del>. ${isPast?'Look at when the action happened.':esc(q.why)} Try again.`}
 else if(isPast&&v===clean(q.contrast)){feedback=`That's the <strong>${q.tense==='preterite'?'imperfect':'preterite'}</strong> form. ${esc(q.why)} Try again.`}
 else if(!isPast){const wrongTense=QUESTIONS.find(other=>other.id!==q.id&&other.verb===q.verb&&other.person===q.person&&clean(other.answer)===v);feedback=wrongTense?`That's a <strong>${esc(wrongTense.tense)}</strong> form. This sentence describes something different in time. Read it again and try another form.`:`Not yet. Think about when the action happens and who does it, then try again.`}
 else feedback=`Not yet. Is this one finished event, or a repeated/background situation? Try another form of <strong>${esc(q.verb)}</strong>.`;
 paintFeedback();}
function paintGuide(){const button=$('v87Guide');if(button)button.textContent=memoryMode?'Go back to guided practice':'Show the next note';const goal=$('v88Goal');if(goal)goal.textContent=showGuide?TUNES[tune].notes[position]:'?';document.querySelectorAll('[data-v87-key]').forEach(b=>b.classList.toggle('v87-target',!!showGuide&&b.dataset.v87Key===TUNES[tune].notes[position]))}
function pressKey(key){sound(key);if(done)return;if(showGuide)helped=true;const t=TUNES[tune],target=t.notes[position];if(key!==target){tries++;logAttempt(false,true);feedback=memoryMode?'That wasn’t the next note. Try listening to the tune again, or return to guided practice.':`You played ${esc(key)}. Look for <strong>${esc(target)}</strong>. ${target.startsWith('C')?'C sits immediately to the left of a pair of black keys.':'Find C first, then count the white keys to '+esc(target)+'.'}`;paintFeedback();return}
 position++;if(position>=t.notes.length){done=true;logAttempt(tries===0&&!helped&&!revealed&&memoryMode&&!guideSeen);feedback=`You played <strong>${esc(t.name)}</strong> all the way through${memoryMode?' from memory':''}. ${memoryMode?'Try another tune, or play this one again tomorrow.':'Ready to try it without the highlighted notes?'}`;}
 else{feedback=memoryMode?'That note was right. Keep going.':`Good. Next note: <strong>${esc(t.notes[position])}</strong>. Keep the rhythm comfortable.`;}
 $('v87Main').querySelector('.v87-melody span').textContent=`Note ${Math.min(position+1,t.notes.length)} of ${t.notes.length}`;paintGuide();paintFeedback()}
function answerEar(answer){if(done)return;const q=EAR[index];if(answer===q.correct){correct(`Right. The second note is <em>${q.correct}</em>. It went from ${esc(q.a)} to ${esc(q.b)}.`,tries===0&&!helped&&!revealed);return}
 tries++;logAttempt(false,true);feedback=`Not quite. Listen once more. Ask yourself whether the second sound sits above, below, or at the same height as the first.`;paintFeedback()}
function hint(){helped=true;if(mode==='spanish'){const q=currentSpanish();feedback=spanishKind==='past'?(q.tense==='preterite'?'Think of a finished event: the preterite.':'Think of a habit or background in the past: the imperfect.'):`${WHY_TENSE[q.tense]} Try the verb for <strong>${esc(q.person)}</strong>.`}
 else if(mode==='piano'){memoryMode=false;showGuide=true;guideSeen=true;paintGuide();feedback=`Look for the highlighted <strong>${esc(TUNES[tune].notes[position])}</strong> key. You can listen to it first.`}
 else{const q=EAR[index],a=PITCHES.find(x=>x.name===q.a).midi,b=PITCHES.find(x=>x.name===q.b).midi;feedback=a===b?'The two sounds are at exactly the same height.':`The second note is ${Math.abs(a-b)} semitone${Math.abs(a-b)===1?'':'s'} away from the first. Listen for its direction.`}paintFeedback()}
function reveal(){revealed=true;if(!done)logAttempt(false);done=true;if(mode==='spanish'){const q=currentSpanish();feedback=`Answer: <strong>${esc(q.answer)}</strong>. ${esc(q.why)}`}else if(mode==='piano'){const t=TUNES[tune];feedback=`The notes are: <strong>${esc(t.notes.join(' · '))}</strong>. Play along, then try the next tune.`;memoryMode=false;showGuide=true;guideSeen=true;paintGuide()}else{const q=EAR[index];feedback=`The second note was <strong>${q.correct}</strong>: ${esc(q.a)} → ${esc(q.b)}.`}paintFeedback()}
function mount(){if($('v87Overlay'))return;const overlay=document.createElement('div');overlay.id='v87Overlay';overlay.innerHTML=`<section id="v87Room" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label="Practice studio"><header><div><span class="v87-eyebrow">STUDY TABLE</span><strong>Practice studio</strong></div><button id="v87Close" type="button" aria-label="Close practice studio">×</button></header><div class="v87-tabs"><button id="v87Tab-spanish" type="button">Spanish in context</button><button id="v87Tab-piano" type="button">Play a tune</button><button id="v87Tab-ear" type="button">Train your ear</button></div><main id="v87Main"></main></section>`;document.body.append(overlay);$('v87Close').onclick=close;overlay.addEventListener('click',e=>{if(e.target===overlay)close()});document.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Escape'&&overlay.classList.contains('open'))close();if(e.key==='Tab'&&overlay.classList.contains('open')){const all=Array.from($('v87Room').querySelectorAll('button:not([disabled]),input:not([disabled])')).filter(x=>x.getClientRects().length);if(!all.length)return;const first=all[0],last=all[all.length-1];if(e.shiftKey&&document.activeElement===first){e.preventDefault();last.focus()}else if(!e.shiftKey&&document.activeElement===last){e.preventDefault();first.focus()}}});
 for(const x of ['spanish','piano','ear'])$('v87Tab-'+x).onclick=()=>{mode=x;reset();if(x==='spanish'){spanishQueue=getQueued(spanishKind==='past'?'past':'spanish');index=spanishQueue.shift()}else if(x==='ear'){earQueue=getQueued('ear');index=earQueue.shift()}else tune=0;render()};
 const mo=new MutationObserver(()=>{const section=document.querySelector('.v65-more-actions');if(section&&!$('v87Launch')){const b=document.createElement('button');b.id='v87Launch';b.type='button';b.textContent='Practice studio: Spanish and piano';b.onclick=()=>open('spanish');section.append(b)}});mo.observe(document.querySelector('#v35Portal')||document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});const s=document.querySelector('.v65-more-actions');if(s&&!$('v87Launch')){const b=document.createElement('button');b.id='v87Launch';b.textContent='Practice studio: Spanish and piano';b.type='button';b.onclick=()=>open('spanish');s.append(b)}
}
window.studyTableV88=window.studyTableV87={open,close,inspect:()=>({mode,index,position,tune,question:mode==='spanish'?currentSpanish():mode==='ear'?EAR[index]:TUNES[tune],progress:load()}),questionCount:QUESTIONS.length,pastCount:PAST_STORIES.length,tuneCount:TUNES.length,earCount:EAR.length};
if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',mount,{once:true});else mount();
})();

<script id="v89-guided-script">

{id:'slope',title:'A line through two points',level:'Start here',intro:'A line passes through (1, 3) and (3, 7). Find its slope, then use it to predict another point.',picture:'line',steps:[
 N('How much does y rise for each 1-unit increase in x?',2,'The rise is 7 − 3 = 4 and the run is 3 − 1 = 2. Slope = rise ÷ run = 2.','Find the change in y and divide it by the change in x.',{'4':'Four is the rise, not the slope. Divide by the horizontal change of 2.','0.5':'You divided run by rise. Slope is rise divided by run.'}),
 N('The line is y = 2x + b. What is b?',1,'Put in the known point (1, 3): 3 = 2(1) + b, so b = 1.','Substitute x = 1 and y = 3.',{'3':'The y-value at x = 1 is not the intercept. Find y when x = 0.','2':'Two is the slope. Subtract it from 3 to get b.'}),
 N('What is y when x = 5?',11,'Use y = 2x + 1. At x = 5, y = 2(5) + 1 = 11.','Use the slope and intercept from the first two steps.',{'10':'You multiplied slope by x but left out the intercept +1.'})]},
{id:'velocity',title:'Speed at one instant',level:'Build on it',intro:'A moving object has position s(t) = t² + 2t metres, with t in seconds. Average speed over an interval is not quite the same as speed at an instant.',picture:'parabola',steps:[
 C('Which derivative gives the instantaneous velocity?',['2t + 2','t² + 2','2t','t + 2'],0,'Differentiate each term: d(t²)/dt = 2t and d(2t)/dt = 2.','A derivative tells you how quickly position changes.', ['Correct.','That still contains a squared term, so you have not differentiated t².','You differentiated t² but forgot that 2t also changes.','The derivative of t² is 2t, not t.']),
 N('How fast is it moving at t = 3 seconds (m/s)?',8,'v(3) = 2(3) + 2 = 8 m/s.','Put t = 3 into 2t + 2.',{'6':'You differentiated the squared term but forgot the extra +2.','15':'Fifteen is the position at t = 3, not the velocity.'}),

The velocity rises from zero, so take half.','12':'That is the speed at the end, not the distance covered.'}),
 N('What was its average speed over those 4 seconds (m/s)?',6,'Average speed = total distance ÷ time = 24 ÷ 4 = 6 m/s.','Use the distance you just calculated.',{'12':'Twelve is the final speed; average speed is lower because it started at zero.'})]},
{id:'chain',title:'What the chain rule changes',level:'Going deeper',intro:'Let y = (2x + 1)³. The outer cube depends on an inner expression, so changing x affects both layers.',steps:[
 N('What is the inner expression 2x + 1 when x = 1?',3,'2(1) + 1 = 3.','Evaluate the parentheses first.'),
 N('For u³, the derivative with respect to u is 3u². What is 3u² when u = 3?',27,'3 × 3² = 27. This is only the outer part of the derivative.','Differentiate the cube first, then insert u = 3.',{'9':'Three squared is 9, but the derivative is 3 times that.','54':'That is the finished derivative after applying the inner factor. This step asks only for the outer factor.'}),
 N('The inner expression has derivative 2. What is dy/dx at x = 1?',54,'Chain rule: 3(2x + 1)² × 2. At x = 1 that is 3 × 9 × 2 = 54.','Multiply the outer derivative from the previous step by the inner derivative 2.',{'27':'You missed the derivative of the inside, which is 2.'})]},

 C('For x ≠ 3, what does the fraction simplify to?',['x − 3','x + 3','x² + 9','3x'],1,'Factor x² − 9 = (x − 3)(x + 3).  N('What is the extra pressure at 5 m depth (pascals)?',50000,'ΔP = 1000 × 10 × 5 = 50,000 Pa.','Pressure added by the water grows linearly with depth.'),
 N('How many times larger is the extra pressure at 5 m than at 2 m?',2.5,'50,000 ÷ 20,000 = 2.5. We compare water-added pressure only, not total absolute pressure.','Divide the deeper pressure by the shallower one.',{'3':'Compare the ratio 5 ÷ 2, not the difference in metres.'})]},
{id:'collision',title:'What sticks in a collision',level:'Going deeper',intro:'A 2 kg cart moving at 3 m/s hits a stationary 1 kg cart. They stick together. Ignore external forces during the collision.',steps:[
 N('What total momentum do they have before impact (kg·m/s)?',6,'Momentum p = mv. The moving cart has 2 × 3 = 6 kg·m/s; the other has zero.','Multiply the moving cart’s mass by speed.',{'9':'Nine is not the total momentum. Momentum is mass times velocity.'}),
 N('How fast does the combined 3 kg mass move afterward (m/s)?',2,'Momentum is conserved: (2 + 1) × v = 6, so v = 2 m/s.','Divide the initial momentum by the combined mass.',{'3':'They are heavier together, so their common speed is lower.'}),
 N('How much kinetic energy was converted to other forms (joules)?',3,'Before: ½ × 2 × 3² = 9 J. After: ½ × 3 × 2² = 6 J. The 3 J difference becomes deformation, heat and sound.','Calculate kinetic energy before and after, then subtract.',{'0':'Momentum is conserved, but kinetic energy is not conserved in a sticking collision.','6':'Six is the kinetic energy afterward, not the energy lost.'})]},
{id:'heat',title:'Heat a cup of water',level:'Going deeper',intro:'A 0.5 kg sample of water has specific heat capacity c = 4200 J/(kg·°C). An ideal heater delivers 700 W entirely to the water.',steps:[
 N('How many joules raise the water temperature by 10 °C?',21000,'Q = mcΔT = 0.5 × 4200 × 10 = 21,000 J.','Multiply mass, specific heat capacity and temperature increase.'),
 N('How many joules are needed for a 20 °C increase?',42000,'Doubling the temperature increase doubles the heat needed: 0.5 × 4200 × 20 = 42,000 J.','Keep mass and c the same; double ΔT.'),
 N('How many seconds must the 700 W heater run for that 20 °C rise?',60,'Time = energy / power = 42,000 / 700 = 60 seconds.','A watt is a joule per second, so divide joules by watts.',{'600':'Divide carefully; 700 × 60 is 42,000.'})]}

const SUBJECTS=[{id:'calculus',label:'Calculus',items:CALC},{id:'physics',label:'Physics',items:PHY},{id:'chess',label:'Chess',items:CHESS}];
const ALL=SUBJECTS.flatMap(s=>s.items.map(i=>({...i,subject:s.id})));
if(new Set(ALL.map(x=>x.subject+'/'+x.id)).size!==ALL.length)throw Error('Repeated lesson ID');
if(ALL.some(x=>x.steps.length!==3))throw Error('Each lesson must have three stages');
const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);const esc=s=>String(s).replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
const now=()=>Date.now();const read=()=>{try{const x=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(KEY)||'{}');return x&&typeof x==='object'&&!Array.isArray(x)?x:{}}catch{return {}}};
function save(v){try{localStorage.setItem(KEY,JSON.stringify(v))}catch{}}
function subject(id){return SUBJECTS.find(x=>x.id===id)||SUBJECTS[0]}
let overlay,sub='calculus',current=null,step=0,finished=false,attempted=false,gotWrong=false,gotHelp=false,chosenOrigin='',currentBoard=null,choiceOrder=null;
let run={help:false,wrong:false,answers:[],startedAt:0};
const pieceName={K:'white king',Q:'white queen',R:'white rook',B:'white bishop',N:'white knight',P:'white pawn',k:'black king',q:'black queen',r:'black rook',b:'black bishop',n:'black knight',p:'black pawn'};
const pieceGlyph={K:'♔',Q:'♕',R:'♖',B:'♗',N:'♘',P:'♙',k:'♚',q:'♛',r:'♜',b:'♝',n:'♞',p:'♟'};
function progress(item){return read()[sub+'/'+item.id]||{attempts:0,clean:0,reviewed:0,misses:0,hints:0}}
function start(item){current=item;step=0;finished=false;attempted=false;gotWrong=false;gotHelp=false;chosenOrigin='';currentBoard=item.board?{...item.board}:null;run={help:false,wrong:false,answers:[],startedAt:now()};render()}
